We introduce the task of experiential intertextuality detection: automatically identifying shared experiential echoes across migration narratives without requiring annotated training data. From 108 French migration narratives spanning both corridors, we automatically generate sentence pairs and score them using annotation-free methods: lexical baselines, sentence embeddings, POS-based structural features, a migration-specific theme lexicon, context-aware narrative features, and zero-shot LLM scoring with Qwen2.5-7B and Mistral-7B under three prompting strategies. We validate all methods against 816 expert-annotated intertextuality judgments (inter-annotator Krippendorff's $α= 0.27$). Our results reveal that all surface, structural, and embedding methods correlate only weakly with expert judgments ($r \leq 0.30$); Qwen2.5-7B zero-shot achieves the best single-method correlation ($r = 0.38$); few-shot examples degrade Qwen but dramatically improve Mistral; narrative position significantly predicts intertextuality, with departure-phase pairs showing the highest experiential echoes; and a supervised hybrid combining all 31 features achieves $r = 0.45$, a 21% improvement over the best individual method.

</details>


### [109] [Hy-MultiTurn: A Six-Dimensional Benchmark for Deep Multi-Turn Dialogue Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29196)
*Eileen Ye,Jiawen Tao,Yaoming Li,Chenxu Liu,Wenhan Yu,Yaxin Fan,Xiaokun Yuan,Mengzhou Wu,Yanbing Jiang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Hy-MultiTurn，一个中文深度多轮对话理解基准，通过分析真实聊天机器人失败案例归纳出六种失败机制，并据此设计六个受控评测模式（约束记忆、精确执行、约束综合、对象定位、行动抑制、指代消解），构建209个12-76轮的长对话任务，评测22个前沿模型配置，证明该基准具有广泛挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多轮对话基准通常只覆盖短对话，未充分评估长多轮交互中的深层理解能力（如记忆早期细节、追踪修订、识别对象和指代、条件抑制），尤其缺乏中文场景下的评测，且对模型失败原因的分析有限。

Method: 首先分析真实聊天机器人失败案例，归纳出六种重复出现的失败机制；基于这些机制设计六个受控评测模式：约束记忆、精确执行、约束综合、对象定位、行动抑制和指代消解；构建209个12-76轮的任务，并加入对话长度、无关话题干扰和口语化表达等难度因素。

Result: 评测22个前沿模型配置后发现：最强配置GPT-5.5也仅在41.1%的响应中满足所有要求，且没有任何模型在全部六个模式中表现最佳，证明Hy-MultiTurn具有广泛挑战性。

Conclusion: Hy-MultiTurn填补了中文长多轮对话深度理解评测的空白，六个受控模式能有效暴露模型的具体失败机制，为改进长对话模型提供了诊断性依据。

Abstract: Long-running multi-turn interactions with chatbots and agents are now common, and a correct response often depends on remembering earlier details, tracking later revisions, identifying intended objects or referents, and withholding action when required conditions are unmet. Existing multi-turn benchmarks typically cover short exchanges and do not fully evaluate these capabilities in long multi-turn interactions, particularly in Chinese, while offering limited insight into how and why models fail. To address these limitations, we analyze real chatbot failures to identify six recurring mechanisms and use them to define six controlled evaluation modes in Hy-MultiTurn, a Chinese benchmark for deep multi-turn dialogue understanding. The six modes evaluate constraint memory, precise execution, constraint synthesis, object localization, action suppression, and reference resolution. Across the six modes, we construct 209 controlled tasks spanning 12-76 turns, with dialogue length, irrelevant-topic distraction, and colloquial phrasing adding further difficulty. Evaluation of 22 frontier model configurations shows that Hy-MultiTurn is broadly challenging, as even GPT-5.5, the strongest overall configuration, satisfies all requirements in only 41.1 percent of responses and no model performs best in all six modes.

</details>


### [110] [Knowing When to Quit: Diagnosing and Training LLMs to Abort Futile Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29211)
*Xinyan Guan,Jiali Zeng,Chunlei Xin,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性地刻画了大语言模型在超出其能力范围的任务上产生"徒劳推理"（futile reasoning）的现象——即生成计算昂贵但语义空洞的推理，并提出了CaRL（能力对齐强化学习）方法，通过奖励塑形和事后拒答增强来将模型行为与能力边界对齐，显著减少徒劳推理同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在超出其能力范围的任务上会产生看似合理但实际错误的推理输出，这种"徒劳推理"现象不仅浪费计算资源，还可能因看似可信的推导误导用户。现有研究缺乏对这一现象的系统性刻画，也缺乏有效的对齐方法来让模型"知道何时该放弃"。

Method: CaRL方法包含两个关键组件：(1) 奖励塑形——通过设计奖励函数激励模型在超出能力边界时选择拒答而非徒劳推理；(2) 事后拒答增强——将模型失败案例转换为拒答监督信号，为模型提供"何时该拒绝"的训练数据。实验覆盖不同难度任务以评估方法的有效性。

Result: 实验表明，CaRL显著减少了徒劳推理现象，同时在不同任务难度上保持了模型性能。模型实现了能力对齐的行为——在能力范围内正常推理，超出能力时拒答，且不牺牲实用性。

Conclusion: 通过系统分析揭示了"徒劳推理"现象的普遍性和能力-行为之间的系统性错配，并证明CaRL能有效实现能力对齐行为，为构建更可靠、更可信的大语言模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models generate computationally expensive yet semantically void reasoning on beyond-capability tasks, creating risks where plausible-sounding but incorrect derivations mislead users. We characterize this \textit{futile reasoning} phenomenon through systematic analysis, revealing universal capability overreach and systematic miscalibration between capability and behavior. The dominant failure mode is specious reasoning, which outputs look superficially valid but contain subtle errors, escalating with task difficulty. To address this, we introduce \textbf{CaRL} (\textbf{Ca}pability-\textbf{a}ligned \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which aligns model behavior with capability boundaries through reward shaping that incentivizes refusal over futile reasoning and hindsight refusal augmentation that converts failures into refusal supervision. Experiments demonstrate a substantial reduction in futile reasoning while preserving performance across task difficulties, effectively achieving capability-aligned behavior without sacrificing utility. \footnote{https://github.com/icip-cas/Knowing-When-to-Quit}

</details>


### [111] [Small Is Enough: Per-User Style Rewriting of AI-Edited Text via LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29238)
*Antorweep Chakravorty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: InMyStyle是一个隐私优先的单用户系统，利用本地辅助LLM将AI编辑的文本改写为用户个人写作风格，无需推理时的指令提示。通过对0.5B到7B参数的小语言模型微调LoRA适配器，在科学论文语料库的219个评估对上，自动综合评分在0.69处趋于平稳，表明小模型足以胜任该改写任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI编辑工具产生的文本往往带有通用化的AI风格，缺乏个性化。同时，基于云端大模型的方案存在隐私泄露风险。作者希望构建一个隐私优先、能适应特定用户写作风格的系统，并探索小模型在该任务上的能力边界。

Method: 系统利用用户提供的文档，通过多个本地辅助LLM构造配对训练样本，然后对0.5B到7B参数的基础模型微调LoRA适配器。推理时无需指令提示，并通过长度感知的生成预算和自动分块来支持不同长度的输入。

Result: 在219个科学论文评估对上，自动综合评分在0.69处对所有模型大小（贪心与采样解码下）达到平稳。400个LLM评判员的评分显示，InMyStyle输出的感知AI分数比辅助AI生成的输入低20%以上，且模型越小感知AI分数越低。

Conclusion: 小模型足以完成所测量的改写任务，模型大小决定的是权衡取舍而非稳定的质量排名。InMyStyle在保持隐私的同时有效降低了输出的AI感，使其更贴近用户个人风格。

Abstract: InMyStyle is a privacy first, single user system that adapts small language models to rewrite AI-edited text towards an individual user's writing style without an instruction prompt at inference. Given a user's documents, it uses multiple local helper LLMs to construct paired training examples and fine tunes LoRA adapters on base models ranging from 0.5B to 7B parameters. Length aware generation budgets and automatic chunking support inputs of different lengths. On 219 evaluation pairs from a scientific-paper corpus, the automatic composite score plateaus at 0.69 [scale 0-1] across all model sizes under both greedy and sampled decoding. This observed plateau suggests that small models are sufficient for the measured rewriting task, with model size determining trade-offs rather than a stable quality ranking. As a secondary evaluation, 400 ratings from five LLM judges give InMyStyle outputs a mean perceived AI-ness score over 20% lower than their helper-AI generated inputs, while mean perceived AI-ness scores decrease with model size within InMyStyle.

</details>


### [112] [Data Turnstile: A Scalable Open Framework for Function-Calling Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29250)
*Goutham Ramakrishnan,Megha Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Data Turnstile，一个开源框架，利用用户定义的API规范生成高质量的函数调用合成训练数据，通过将多轮工具交互分解为带验证和错误反馈循环的分步生成过程，有效提升小语言模型（SLM）在工具使用任务上的性能，在多个基准上显著超越基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在智能体部署中具有低延迟、低成本、设备端隐私等优势，但在工具使用任务上表现不佳。与大型模型不同，SLM无法通过模型容量弥补低质量监督数据的不足，因此数据质量成为关键瓶颈，需要专门为SLM生成高质量的函数调用训练数据。

Method: Data Turnstile框架将多轮工具使用交互分解为约束化、逐步生成的过程，并引入验证和错误反馈循环。该方法对API多样性、对话复杂度和输出正确性提供细粒度控制，从而生成高质量合成训练数据。具体包括：基于用户定义API规范生成数据、分步构建多轮交互、通过验证和反馈机制确保数据质量。

Result: 在BFCL单轮基准上，Qwen3-0.6B微调后达到75.9%准确率（基础模型仅67.4%），接近和超越更大模型。在τ²-bench多轮基准上，Turnstile训练的Qwen3-1.7B在电信领域达到31.1% pass^1，比基线提升4.7倍，超过19倍大的Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct（27.4%）。数据集覆盖1000+ API和10万+多轮交互。

Conclusion: Data Turnstile证明了通过高质量合成数据可以有效弥补SLM在工具使用任务上的能力差距，使小模型在特定领域适应中能够超越远大于自身的模型，展示了数据质量而非模型容量才是SLM在agentic任务中的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are attractive for agentic deployment due to low latency, reduced cost, and on-device privacy, yet they struggle with tool-use tasks where training data is scarce and noisy. Unlike larger models, SLMs cannot compensate for low-quality supervision through sheer capacity, making data quality the critical bottleneck. We present Data Turnstile, an open-source framework that takes user-defined API specifications and generates high-quality synthetic training data for function calling. Turnstile decomposes multi-turn tool-use interactions into constrained, stepwise generation with validation and error-feedback loops, providing fine-grained control over API diversity, conversation complexity, and output correctness. We demonstrate effectiveness of domain adaptation with Turnstile data on two challenging function calling benchmarks. On the BFCL single-turn benchmark, a Qwen3-0.6B fine-tuned on Turnstile data without chain-of-thought achieves 75.9% overall accuracy (versus 67.4% for the base model with thinking enabled), closing the gap with thinking-enabled Qwen3-1.7B (78.4%) and Qwen3-4B (79.9%) despite being 3$\times$ and 7$\times$ smaller respectively. On $τ^2$-bench, a multi-turn agentic benchmark, Turnstile-trained Qwen3-1.7B achieves 31.1% pass^1 on the Telecom domain, improving 4.7$\times$ over its 6.6% base and surpassing Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct (27.4%), a model 19$\times$ larger. Turnstile-trained Qwen3-0.6B achieves 24.6%, improving 7$\times$ over its 3.5% base and approaching the 32B model (53$\times$ larger). We release Data Turnstile along with a dataset spanning 1,000+ APIs and 100K+ multi-turn interactions.

</details>


### [113] [CalibratedRubric: Task-Adaptive Rubric Banks for Open-Ended LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29252)
*Mengting Chen,Yanshu Sun,Wanting Liang,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Dezhi Chen,Jing Li,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CalibratedRubric是一个任务自适应的开放式LLM评估框架，通过类型特异评分、基于Beta-Bernoulli后验的可测性过滤和IRT驱动的子模信息覆盖集构建，仅用49个评分标准（而非131个）即可在多个基准上提升人类-金牌一致性（JudgmentBench κ从0.604提升到0.743）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式LLM输出的可靠评估需要细粒度评分标准，但专家人工制定昂贵且难以扩展。现有自动化流程依赖严格的评判一致性和二元方差过滤，无法区分可测量的评分标准与信息量大的评分标准，缺乏不确定性感知。

Method: 提出CalibratedRubric框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 类型特异评分（type-specific scoring）；2) 基于Beta-Bernoulli协议后验的贝叶斯评分标准可测性过滤；3) 基于项目反应理论（IRT）的评分标准库组装，通过子模信息覆盖目标在观测能力范围内构建紧凑的评分标准集合。

Result: 在金融、医疗、通用和法律基准上，可测性过滤使JudgmentBench上的人-金一致性从κ=0.604提升至0.743。IRT贪心选择在所有六个评估响应块上优于随机选择的交叉拟合秩保真度，且仅需49个而非131个评分标准即可达到FinResearchBench决策支持任务的目标相关性。

Conclusion: CalibratedRubric是一种高效且不确定性感知的开放式LLM评估方法。校准增益依赖于足够的评判冗余，任务标签扰动进一步降低了系统分离度，证实了任务自适应评分的实际意义。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of open-ended LLM outputs requires fine-grained rubrics, yet expert curation is costly and difficult to scale. Existing automated pipelines rely on strict judge unanimity and binary variance filters, which cannot distinguish measurable rubrics from informative ones. We introduce CalibratedRubric, a task-adaptive framework that combines type-specific scoring, Bayesian rubric-measurability filtering, and item response theory (IRT)-based bank assembly. CalibratedRubric estimates each rubric's measurability with a Beta--Bernoulli agreement posterior and uses a submodular information-coverage objective to construct compact rubric banks over the observed capability range. Across financial, healthcare, general, and legal benchmarks, measurability filtering improves human-gold agreement on JudgmentBench from $κ=0.604$ to $0.743$. IRT-based greedy selection improves cross-fitted rank fidelity over random selection across all six evaluated response blocks and requires only 49 rather than 131 rubrics to reach the target correlation on FinResearchBench decision-support tasks. Task-label perturbations further reduce system separation, confirming the practical relevance of task-adaptive scoring. These results support CalibratedRubric as an efficient, uncertainty-aware approach to open-ended LLM evaluation, with calibration gains depending on sufficient judge redundancy.

</details>


### [114] [Translation with Thought: Difficulty-Adaptive Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Domain Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29287)
*Yongshi Ye,Biao Fu,Chongxuan Huang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TwT（Translation with Thought），一个资源理性的翻译框架，通过区分直觉推理和深思推理来适应不同领域翻译任务的难度差异。在15个基准和59种语言上，TwT-7B和TwT-14B在翻译质量上超越更大的推理模型，同时减少32-60%的token使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域机器翻译（MDMT）面临不同领域语言复杂度差异大的挑战，而现有方法没有考虑翻译推理成本与难度之间的平衡。人类译者会依据难度灵活调整推理努力，但当前的机器翻译模型缺乏这种资源理性的自适应机制。

Method: TwT采用两阶段训练：(1) 监督微调阶段，使用从DeepSeek-R1蒸馏并经GPT-4o重写为符合人类推理经济性的难度感知长链思维轨迹；(2) 强化学习阶段，采用混合奖励同时优化翻译质量和推理效率，实现推理强度与任务难度的动态匹配。

Result: 在15个基准（涵盖领域内和领域外设置）、3种已知和59种未见语言上评估，并在三个骨干模型上进行消融实验。TwT-7B和TwT-14B在翻译质量上优于更大的SOTA推理模型，同时减少32-60%的token使用量。

Conclusion: 将翻译行为与认知原则对齐（即根据难度调节推理努力）能同时实现MDMT中的鲁棒泛化、高质量翻译和高效推理，验证了资源理性框架在机器翻译中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-domain machine translation (MDMT) poses a unique challenge due to varying levels of linguistic complexity across domains. Inspired by human translators' ability to adapt reasoning effort based on difficulty, we propose TwT (Translation with Thought), a resource-rational framework that learns to modulate inference between intuitive and deliberate reasoning. TwT is trained in two stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on difficulty-aware long chain-of-thought traces distilled from DeepSeek-R1 and rewritten by GPT-4o to reflect human-like reasoning economy, and (2) reinforcement learning with a hybrid reward to optimize translation quality and reasoning efficiency. Evaluated on 15 benchmarks spanning in-domain and out-of-domain settings, as well as 3 seen and 59 unseen languages, with ablations across three backbone models, TwT-7B and TwT-14B outperform much larger SOTA reasoning models in translation quality, while reducing token usage by 32--60\%. These results confirm that aligning translation behavior with cognitive principles enables robust generalization, high translation quality, and efficient reasoning in MDMT.

</details>


### [115] [Cross-Lingual Transfer for Machine Translation in Turkic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29355)
*Omer Burak Cinar,Mehmet Mert Dalkilic,Cagri Toraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了五个突厥语系语言（土耳其语、阿塞拜疆语、乌兹别克语、哈萨克语、吉尔吉斯语）之间的跨语言迁移行为，通过成对迁移矩阵发现在密切相关的语言对（如土耳其语-阿塞拜疆语、哈萨克语-吉尔吉斯语）之间迁移效果最强，且迁移方向、翻译目标和拉丁化处理都会对迁移效果产生影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言迁移是低资源机器翻译的核心问题，但现有研究对密切相关的语系内部迁移行为缺乏深入刻画。理解同一语系内语言间的迁移规律，有助于为低资源语言选择更优的迁移来源和策略。

Method: 采用成对迁移矩阵方法，在mT5模型上对五个突厥语系语言进行实验。每个模型使用一个迁移源语言进行微调，在不同的迁移目标语言上进行评估，同时保持翻译目标不变。额外分析考察了拉丁化处理（Latinization）对脚本不匹配场景的影响，以及迁移源在不同数据集和模型设置下的稳定性。

Result: 发现：1) 密切相关语言对（土耳其-阿塞拜疆、哈萨克-吉尔吉斯）迁移效果最强；2) 迁移方向不对称，源-目标对在不同翻译目标下表现不同；3) 拉丁化在部分脚本不匹配场景下提升BLEU和chrF，但效果并非在所有指标上一致；4) 迁移源在不同数据集和模型设置下基本保持稳定。

Conclusion: 跨语言迁移在突厥语系内存在显著的语言亲缘性和方向性效应，迁移源的稳定性为低资源语言选择迁移配置提供了实用指导，但拉丁化等预处理手段的效果需根据具体指标谨慎评估。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer is central to low-resource machine translation, but its behavior within closely related language families remains insufficiently characterized. We study transfer among five Turkic languages; Turkish, Azerbaijani, Uzbek, Kazakh, and Kyrgyz; using pairwise transfer matrices. In this setting, each model is fine-tuned with one transfer source and evaluated on a different transfer target while the translation target remains the same. Across mT5 experiments, we find that transfer is strongest between closely related Turkic pairs, especially Turkish-Azerbaijani and Kazakh-Kyrgyz. We also show that transfer direction matters, and that the same transfer source-transfer target pair can behave differently when the translation target changes. Latinization improves BLEU and chrF in several script-mismatched settings, but its effect is not uniform across metrics. Additional analyses show that transfer sources are mostly stable across different datasets and model settings.

</details>


### [116] [Zero-Mem: Zero-Token Memory Operations for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29377)
*Yilin Xiao,Zhehan Zhu,Yujing Zhang,Jin Chen,Zijin Hong,Luyao Zhuang,Qinggang Zhang,Shengyuan Chen,Xiaocao Ouyang,Lingfei Ren,Xiao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Zero-Mem提出了一种零token记忆操作机制，使LLM智能体在长期交互中无需任何额外的LLM调用或token消耗即可实现结构化记忆访问。它通过实体-上下文图与时间层级两种互补方式组织原始交互痕迹，并结合确定性校准为最终QA阅读器提供支撑证据。在长记忆与长上下文QA基准上取得有竞争力的性能，同时将记忆操作的时间成本降低了57.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的记忆系统通常依赖额外的LLM调用来生成中间记录和检索中介，这会带来持续的token和时间开销，且遗漏或合并的细节可能丢失原始证据。作者希望探索是否可以在不进行任何额外生成的情况下实现结构化记忆访问。

Method: Zero-Mem采用零token记忆操作，不使用除最终QA阅读器之外的任何LLM调用。它保留原始交互痕迹作为记录来源，并通过两种互补方式组织：实体-上下文图暴露跨交互的连接，时间层级保留对话局部性和会话状态。查询时，系统权衡两种视图并分别检索，遵循其结构恢复支持关系或上下文；确定性校准丢弃冲突证据，确保阅读器答案有据可依。

Result: 在长记忆和长上下文QA基准上，Zero-Mem实现了有竞争力的性能，同时消除了记忆操作中的LLM调用和token消耗。在相同的最终QA阅读器和上下文预算下，相较最快的基线，记忆操作时间成本降低57.6%。消融实验验证了两个视图及其查询依赖协调的贡献。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结构化智能体记忆无需生成过去的中间表示，零token记忆操作是一种可行且高效的方法，为LLM智能体的长期记忆设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM agents need memory to act consistently over long interactions, yet many systems use additional LLM calls to operate that memory. Generating intermediate records and mediating their retrieval adds recurring token and time costs, while omitted or merged details can obscure the original evidence. We ask whether structured memory access requires generation at all. Zero-Mem introduces \emph{zero-token memory operations}: no step outside final question answering invokes an LLM or consumes LLM input or output tokens; encoder computation is accounted for separately. Zero-Mem preserves original interaction traces as its source of record. It organizes the traces in two complementary ways. An entity--context graph exposes connections across interactions, while a temporal hierarchy preserves conversational locality and session state. For each query, Zero-Mem weighs the two views, retrieves from both, and follows their structure to recover supporting relations or surrounding context. Deterministic calibration first discards conflicting evidence and then keeps the reader's answer grounded in the retrieved traces. Only the final-QA reader invokes an LLM. Across long-memory and long-context question-answering benchmarks, Zero-Mem achieves competitive performance while eliminating LLM calls and LLM-token consumption from memory operations. With the same final-QA reader and context budget, it reduces memory-operation time cost by 57.6\% relative to the fastest compared baseline. Ablations support the contribution of the two views and their query-dependent coordination. Overall, the results show that structured agent memory need not generate an intermediate representation of the past. After peer review, the code and implementation details will be available at \textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/TheMoon0815/Zero-mem}.

</details>


### [117] [PTP: Previous-Token Prediction based LLM Inversion for Near-Exact Prompt Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29378)
*Pirzada Suhail,Nagasai Saketh Naidu,Atanu R Sinha,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种黑盒环境下的大语言模型逆映射方法，通过从目标LLM自身合成数据训练一个完全从头开始的显式逆语言模型，利用前一个token预测（previous-token prediction）进行训练，实现无需辅助工具或模型权重的忠实提示重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往LLM逆映射工作将提示恢复视为语义重建任务，依赖微调预训练的序列到序列模型、大规模外部数据集以及模型权重或logits的访问权限。这些方法存在依赖性强、适用范围有限的问题，缺少一种纯粹黑盒、无需外部辅助的功能性逆映射方法。

Method: 1) 从目标LLM自身合成数据，训练一个完全从零开始的显式逆语言模型；2) 类比正向next-token预测，逆模型使用previous-token预测进行训练，建立正逆过程的生成性联系；3) 通过采样天然支持多样化的提示重构，且所有重构提示在前向目标LLM下能产生相似响应。

Result: 该方法在多个数据集上具有泛化能力，并展现出跨不同LLM生成响应时重构提示的可迁移性。在token级评估指标上，提示和响应重构均优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 本文通过纯黑盒、无辅助手段的功能性逆映射框架，实现了忠实且多样的提示重构，并在多个评估维度上超越了现有方法，为LLM反向推理提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text by auto-regressively sampling the next token. This inherently leads to a many-to-many mapping between prompts and responses, complicating the task of inferring prompts from observed outputs. Prior work on LLM inversion frames prompt recovery as a semantic reconstruction task. They rely on fine-tuning pretrained sequence-to-sequence models on large external datasets--and requiring access to model weights or logits--to generate semantically plausible prompts. In contrast, we present a functional approach to inverting a given LLM in a black-box setting, without auxiliary aids. We train an explicit inverse language model entirely from scratch on data synthetically generated from the target LLM itself. Analogous to forward next-token prediction, our inverse model is trained using previous-token prediction, establishing a generative link between the forward and inverse processes that enables faithful prompt reconstruction. Moreover, it naturally supports diverse prompt reconstructions through sampling, whereby all such prompts induce similar responses under the forward, target LLM. Our approach generalises across datasets and exhibits transferability in reconstructing prompts from responses generated by different LLMs. Further, across the set of token based evaluation metrics for prompt and response reconstructions, our approach outperforms prior work.

</details>


### [118] [Studying quantization trade-offs for efficient inference deployment in machine translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29397)
*Jim Zhao,Sohir Maskey,Koen Oostermeijer,Douglas Orr,Teryn Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在真实服务器环境中的量化部署问题，重点分析了EuroLLM和Hy-MT2两个翻译模型家族（1.7B到22B）在单一A100/H100 GPU上的量化权衡。研究发现，将文档分块策略与W4A8/W8A8量化相结合可改善延迟-吞吐量帕累托曲线，并提出了基于WMT24++的文档级评估方法来揭示标准段级评估无法捕捉的量化与长上下文交互问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究很少在受控的编排级工作负载下评估量化对延迟和吞吐量的影响，标准MT基准依赖孤立句子无法捕捉长上下文动态，需要更全面的评估框架来指导量化部署决策。

Method: 研究采用了两种翻译模型家族（EuroLLM和Hy-MT2）覆盖1.7B到22B五个规模，在A100/H100 GPU上进行W4A8和W8A8量化，结合文档分块策略评估延迟-吞吐量权衡，并提出基于WMT24++的文档级评估来衡量分块策略对翻译质量的影响。

Result: Hy-MT2在量化下表现稳健，而EuroLLM对量化高度敏感，翻译质量快速崩溃。标准段级评估无法预测量化和长上下文文档翻译之间的交互，推断效率与翻译质量的权衡不仅取决于量化格式，还取决于文本分块策略的选择。

Conclusion: 量化部署的优化需要综合考虑量化格式和文本分块策略，文档级评估对于准确评估量化模型的实际性能至关重要，不同模型对量化的鲁棒性差异显著。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in realistic server environments poses challenges, as the system needs to provide high-quality responses with low latency. Quantization is a common approach to reduce the memory footprint and improve inference efficiency, yet its impact on latency and throughput is rarely evaluated under controlled, orchestration-level workloads. In this work we study the quantization trade-offs of two translation model families, EuroLLM \citep{martins2025eurollm} and Hy-MT2 \citep{zheng2026hy} across five models ranging from 1.7B to 22B for efficient deployment on a single A100 or H100 GPU. We demonstrate that combining a document-chunking strategy with W4A8 or W8A8 quantization improves the latency-throughput Pareto-curve under a wide range of workloads. Furthermore, since standard machine translation (MT) benchmarks rely on isolated sentences and fail to capture long-context dynamics, we introduce a document-level evaluation from WMT24++ to assess how text chunking strategies affect translation quality under quantization. Our results reveal that standard segment-level evaluation can fail to predict the interaction between quantization and long-context document translation. While Hy-MT2 remains robust under quantization, EuroLLM shows strong sensitivity and translation quality collapses rapidly for all considered quantization formats. Overall, our experiments show that the trade-off between inference efficiency and translation quality depends not only on the quantization format, but also on the choice of text chunking strategy.

</details>


### [119] [Know It, Act on It: Investigating Memory Utilization in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29433)
*Zhaoxin Feng,Jianfei Ma,Emmanuele Chersoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦评估范式，将LLM代理的记忆能力拆分为"知道"（Know）和"行动"（Act）两个独立测试，以隔离"遗忘"与"记住但未使用"的知识利用问题。通过对16个系统、5种记忆架构、1000条用户偏好的大规模实验，发现代理经常能通过记忆召回测试（Know）但在对应行为场景（Act）中无法应用该偏好，且健康与治疗相关的偏好利用能力最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理作为个性化助手时，即使相关的用户偏好完整出现在上下文中，模型也可能未能据此调整响应。现有方法无法区分代理是"没记住"还是"记住了但没使用"该信息，这阻碍了对记忆能力的准确诊断和改进。

Method: 提出解耦评估范式：对同一条用户偏好分别施配对的Know测试（记忆召回）和Act测试（行为应用），在两个层面独立评估。通过16个系统和5种记忆架构进行大规模实验，评估1000条嵌入三种表达强度级别的用户偏好。

Result: 发现Know与Act结果之间存在显著差距：代理经常通过用户偏好的召回测试，但在配对的偏好行为场景中无法体现该偏好。记忆架构虽能缩小这一差距，但在健康和治疗相关偏好的情境下，利用失败最为严重——而这些恰恰是现实世界中影响最大的场景。

Conclusion: LLM代理的记忆问题不仅是"记不记得住"，更是"记得住会不会用"。当前记忆架构对消除知识利用差距的帮助有限，尤其在健康与治疗等高风险领域，需要专门改善代理在行为层面对已有偏好的应用能力。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents evolve into personalized companions, memory has emerged as a core capability. However, LLMs face a knowledge utilization problem: they may fail to act on relevant user preferences even when they are fully present in context. When an agent fails to tailor its response in a context where previously shared user preferences should matter, it is unclear whether the model failed to remember that information or remembered it but failed to use it. To isolate this breakdown, we introduce a decoupled evaluation paradigm that administers paired Know and Act tests to the same user preference. We conduct large-scale experiments across 16 systems and five memory architectures, evaluating 1,000 preferences embedded at three levels of expression strength. Our results show a large gap between Know and Act outcomes: agents often pass the recall test for a user preference but fail to reflect that same preference in the paired behavioral scenario. While memory architectures reduce this gap, utilization remains especially weak for health and therapy-related preferences, where failures to act carry the greatest real-world stakes.

</details>


### [120] [Evidence-Type Competition: When Can Interventional Data Teach Language Models Causal Direction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29484)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过全合成环境（辛普森悖论世界）检验"干预数据是训练因果推理黄金标准"的假设，发现该假设失败：干预样本比例不决定模型do()响应的方向，方向由推理时上下文中存在的证据类型决定。观测上下文会抑制因果插值能力，而擦除观测证据可立即释放该能力。能力存储在权重中，开关在上下文中，激活修补定位到中间层的观测行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干预数据（interventional data）被广泛认为是教模型因果推理的金标准，作者希望在一个完全受控的合成环境中检验这一假设是否成立——即当观测相关性与因果效应系统性相反（辛普森悖论世界）时，训练中干预样本的比例是否能真正提升模型的因果方向判断能力。

Method: 在辛普森悖论世界中进行对照实验：改变预训练中干预样本的比例；在推理时区分纯观测上下文、混合上下文和对齐干预探针三种情境；通过擦除观测证据、四状态内容操控验证机制；使用激活修补（activation patching）定位因果开关位置；在CLadder上进行外部审计，包括符号随机重训练；量化基于探针的因果评测的采样噪声底限，并提出证据平均协议。

Result: 增加干预样本比例只增加do()响应幅度但不改变符号方向（符号复制自观测上下文）。纯观测上下文29/50世界产生系统性符号反转，混合上下文19/50，对齐干预探针41/50正确。擦除观测证据立即使因果插值能力释放（ratio_true=+0.56）。抑制在11/11训练种子中稳定，在0.93B参数下依然稳健（31.8% vs 6%反转率）。CLadder审计显示所学正效应先验具有两层结构，符号随机重训练可移除非分布内但非分布外。证据平均协议将符号错误从26%降至9%。

Conclusion: 模型已习得因果推理能力（存在于权重中），但该能力是否被调用的开关位于推理时的上下文中——具体由中间层的观测证据行控制。训练数据混合比例不是决定性因素，评测时上下文中的证据类型才是关键。这挑战了"更多干预数据=更好因果推理"的朴素假设，提示需要重新思考因果能力评测协议。

Abstract: Interventional data is widely regarded as the gold standard for teaching models causal reasoning. We test this assumption in a fully controlled synthetic environment pitting observational correlation against causal effect, and find it fails instructively. In Simpson's-paradox worlds, where the two have systematically opposite signs, increasing the fraction of interventional samples in pretraining does not improve causal direction: the magnitude of the model's do()-response grows monotonically, yet its sign is copied from the observational context. What governs whether interventional evidence is used is not the training mixture but the evidence type present in the context at inference time. Under an identical training recipe, a purely observational context induces systematic sign reversal in 29/50 worlds, a mixed context in 19/50, while aligned interventional probes alone yield 41/50 correct. Erasing observational evidence from the context immediately releases the suppressed causal interpolation ability (ratio_true = +0.56); a four-state content manipulation shows the switch is content-mediated and graded. The suppression is stable across training seeds (11/11 strong reversals persist on a matched-protocol second seed) and robust as a rate at 0.93B parameters (31.8% vs. 6% reversals in the matched probe-only arm), even as absolute gains shrink four-fold. An external audit on CLadder exposes a learned positive-effect prior with a two-layer structure: sign-randomized retraining removes it in-distribution but not out-of-distribution. We summarize: the capability lives in the weights; the switch lives in the context, and activation patching localizes the switch to the middle layers' observational rows. We further quantify the sampling noise floor of probe-based causal evaluation and an evidence-averaging protocol that cuts sign errors from 26% to 9%.

</details>


### [121] [ARB: A Matched Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark Dataset for AI-Text Detector Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29539)
*Gaetano Perrone,Simon Pietro Romano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB)，构建了包含1,800个人类源文本和四种开源LLM生成器的重写基准，评估五个检测器在不同重写场景下的性能。结果显示，检测器在直接LLM文本上的高召回率（90%+）在人类文本被LLM重写后骤降至15-31%，但在LLM文本被同模型重写时保持较高性能（78-83%），表明传统人机对比基准无法预测检测器在人类文本重写场景下的实际表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测基准仅比较人类写作与LLM直接生成文本，忽略了人类文本被LLM重写这一实际场景。虽然已知改写会降低检测性能，但传统基准的指标是否能够预测检测器在人类内容被LLM重写时的行为，仍是一个未解问题。

Method: 构建ARB基准，包含1,800个人类源文本（分别来自XSum、WritingPrompts、OpenWebText各600篇），使用四种开源生成器（Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B），每个源文本生成四种匹配变体：人类写作、LLM直接生成、LLM重写人类文本、同模型LLM重写LLM文本。在1%假阳性率的严格操作点评估五个检测器（FastDetectGPT、Binoculars-falcon-7b、RADAR、BERT-Defense、RoBERTa-Defense）的TPR。

Result: FastDetectGPT和Binoculars在直接LLM文本检测上达到91.2%和93.5%召回率，但在人类文本被LLM重写后骤降至30.8%和15.1%（下降60-78个百分点）。而LLM文本被同模型重写时，两者仍保持78.3%和83.0%召回率（仅下降10-13个百分点）。RADAR呈现相同模式（66.8%降至12.2%），BERT-Defense和RoBERTa-Defense在所有场景下召回率均低于3%。

Conclusion: 传统人机对比基准的性能指标无法迁移到人类文本被LLM重写的场景。检测器在LLM改写LLM文本时保持鲁棒，但在改写人类文本时性能急剧下降，说明现有检测方法未能捕捉人类写作被LLM修改后的特征变化。

Abstract: Standard AI-text detection benchmarks compare human-written text against text generated directly by large language models (LLMs). While prior work has shown that rewriting and paraphrasing can degrade detector performance, it remains unclear whether performance measured on this conventional benchmark predicts detector behavior when human-authored content is rewritten by an LLM. To address this gap, we introduce Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB), built from 1,800 human source texts (600 each from XSum, WritingPrompts, and OpenWebText) and four open-weight generators (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B). Each source item yields four matched variants: human-written (HUMAN), direct LLM generation (Free-LLM), LLM-rewritten human text (H2L), and same-generator LLM-rewritten LLM text (LLM2L). We evaluated five detectors (FastDetectGPT, Binoculars-falcon-7b, RADAR, BERT-Defense, RoBERTa-Defense) at a strict 1%-false-positive operating point (TPR@1%FPR). FastDetectGPT and Binoculars-falcon-7b detected 91.2% and 93.5\% of direct LLM text, but only 30.8% and 15.1% of human text an LLM had rewritten, a drop of 60-78 percentage points. The same detectors retained 78.3% and 83.0% recall when LLM text was rewritten by the same model, a much smaller decline of 10-13 points. RADAR followed the same pattern (66.8% to 12.2%), while BERT-Defense and RoBERTa-Defense stayed below 3% recall across all regimes. These results show that detector performance measured on the conventional human-vs-LLM benchmark does not transfer to human-authored text revised by an LLM, even though the same detectors remain largely robust to LLM-only rewriting.

</details>


### [122] [Sycophancy Undermines Epistemic Vigilance in Cooperative Vision-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29585)
*Rupak Sarkar,Neha Srikanth,Saloni Gupta,Claire Bonial,Philip Resnik,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于信息不对称对话的"找不同"任务来评估视觉-语言模型在合作场景中的认知警觉性（epistemic vigilance）。研究发现模型在对话中经常忽视自己私有图像中的关键证据而盲从对话伙伴，表现出过度迎合（sycophancy）的行为。作者证明通过任务无关的sycophancy示例学习到的steering向量可以有效减少这类错误，使模型更忠实地报告自己的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类合作对话中，参与者需要具备认知警觉性——当新信息与已有信念冲突时能察觉并修复冲突。AI系统要成为复杂合作任务中可靠的伙伴，也必须具备类似能力。然而现有研究缺乏对视觉-语言模型在合作设置中认知警觉性的系统性评估。

Method: 提出了一个信息不对称的基于对话的"找不同"（spot-the-difference）任务：两个模型各自私下看到一张图片，通过对话判断两图是否相同或找出差异。该任务设计用于衡量VLM在合作对话中的认知警觉性，并将其违背行为与sycophancy联系起来，最后使用从任务无关sycophancy示例学习的steering向量来干预模型行为。

Result: 模型在该任务中经常失败：倾向于同意对话伙伴而忽视自己私有图像中的关键证据，即使这种同意是没有根据的。使用sycophancy steering向量可以减少认知警觉性相关错误，使模型更忠实地报告自己的证据。

Conclusion: 认知警觉性的违背与sycophancy密切相关，表现为合作目标导向对话中的过度迎合和薄弱证据支撑。通过steering减少sycophancy可以提升模型在信息不对称合作任务中的可靠性，使其成为更值得信赖的对话伙伴。

Abstract: To maintain common ground in cooperative conversation, humans iteratively update their beliefs as conversation participants share new information; participants who are epistemically vigilant detect when new information conflicts with prior beliefs and take steps to repair these conflicts. In order for AI systems to serve as reliable partners in complex cooperative tasks, they must similarly weigh incoming information against their own private evidence and shared context and appropriately surface inconsistencies when they arise. To measure the epistemic vigilance of vision-language models in cooperative settings, we present an information-asymmetric, dialog-based "spot-the-difference" task. Two models are privately shown one image each, and must determine through conversation whether the images are identical or, if not, identify the difference. Models routinely fail at this: they frequently overlook key evidence in their private image in favor of agreeing with their conversational partner, even when their agreement is unwarranted. We relate these violations of epistemic vigilance to the broader behavior of sycophancy, which manifests itself in cooperative goal-oriented dialog as over-accommodation and weak evidential grounding. Our results show that model steering to reduce sycophancy with a vector learned from task-agnostic sycophancy examples can reduce epistemic vigilance-related errors, making models more faithful reporters of their evidence, and in turn, more reliable partners in information-asymmetric cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [123] [ResKV: Reconstructing Omitted Attention Contributions for Fixed-Budget KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29591)
*Yuhang Zhan,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ResKV是一种KV缓存压缩方法，将固定KV预算划分为精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存，残差缓存重建被驱逐token的贡献。残差条目与主缓存token参与相同的softmax归一化，同时恢复注意力的分子和分母质量。通过构建期验证代理和推近期动态门控进行优化，在LongBench和RULER上展示广泛改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法存在根本性缺陷：驱逐方法永久丢弃未选token，丧失其聚合注意力贡献；合并类方法虽保留更多信息，但会扰动本应保持精确的保留key和value。需要一种既能保留完整注意力贡献又不过度扰动精确缓存的方法。

Method: ResKV将固定KV预算分为两部分：(1) 精确主缓存，保留未被驱逐的token；(2) 紧凑残差缓存，用残差统计量重构被丢弃token的贡献。残差条目与主缓存token参与同一softmax归一化，同时恢复注意力的分子和分母质量。构建期使用验证代理决定每层和每个KV头的残差分配，推近期用动态门控根据单个query调整残差贡献。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER上进行了全面评估，覆盖query感知和query无关设置、多种骨干网络、不同缓存预算和代表性压缩基线。在相同保留KV预算下展示广泛改进，同时保持压缩解码的实际效率，包括峰值内存使用和长上下文解码吞吐量。

Conclusion: ResKV通过精确主缓存与残差缓存的结合，有效解决了驱逐方法丢失信息和合并方法扰动精确token的问题，在保持压缩解码效率的同时实现更优的推理质量。

Abstract: KV cache compression is essential for efficient long-context inference. Existing eviction methods permanently discard unselected tokens and consequently remove their aggregate contribution to attention. Merging-based alternatives preserve more information but can perturb retained keys and values that should remain exact. We observe that the information omitted by cache eviction can be formulated as residual statistics in both the numerator and denominator of softmax attention. Based on this observation, we propose ResKV, which divides a fixed KV budget into an exact main cache and a compact residual cache that reconstructs the contribution of omitted tokens. ResKV lets main-cache tokens and residual entries participate in the same softmax normalization, so residual entries restore both attention numerator and denominator mass rather than acting as a post-hoc correction. A construction-time validation proxy determines residual allocation for each layer and KV head, while a decode-time dynamic gate adjusts residual contributions for individual queries. Comprehensive evaluations on LongBench and RULER, covering query-aware and query-agnostic settings, multiple backbones, cache budgets, and representative compression baselines, demonstrate broad improvements under the same retained KV budget while preserving the practical efficiency of compressed decoding, including peak memory usage and long-context decode throughput.

</details>


### [124] [FriendBench: Benchmarking Dyadic Familiarity Inference in Humans and Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29602)
*Jeffrey M. Girard,Jason Z. Zheng,Jacqueline R. Vertino,Antony D'Avirro,Benjamin Peloquin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FriendBench是一个用于从20秒双人破冰对话片段推断两人是否熟识的基准测试。实验比较了26个模型（来自7家公司）与人类评审在三种模态（文本、音频、视频）上的表现，发现最佳模型与人类在准确率上无统计显著差异，但决策方式不同：人类在两类回答间保持平衡，而模型偏向"陌生人"答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解社会情境往往依赖于非语言行为，而现有基准大多聚焦于语言内容本身。作者希望探究模型是否能从互动方式（而非对话内容）推断人际关系状态，并系统比较模型与人类在这项社交推断任务上的表现差异。

Method: 构建FriendBench基准，包含96个平衡的双人破冰对话对（20秒片段），所有配对回答相同类型的提示词，确保只能通过互动方式推断答案。在文本、音频、视频三种模态下，对26个模型（来自7家公司）与匹配的人类评审小组进行对比评估。

Result: 最佳模型与人类评审在三种模态下的准确率均无统计显著差异，但模型倾向于回答"陌生人"（更偏向该类别），而人类保持两类答案的平衡，这反映的是有效先验差异而非判别能力差异。更丰富的通道（如视频）对模型和人类的提升不均衡，只有人类能从可见行为中获益。

Conclusion: 尽管最强模型在准确率上可与人类匹敌，但它们在决策偏好和利用多模态信息的方式上与人类存在本质差异。模型应改进其先验校准，并更好地利用视觉行为信息。作者公开了刺激材料、人类评分和模型预测，以推动该方向的研究。

Abstract: Reading a social situation often depends on behavior, not words alone. We introduce FriendBench, a benchmark for inferring whether two people are already familiar or are meeting as strangers, from a 20-second clip of a dyadic ice-breaker conversation. Every pair answers the same type of prompt, so only the manner of interaction can reveal the answer. Across text, audio, and video, we compare 26 models from seven companies against matched human panels over 96 balanced dyads. The best model and the human crowd are statistically indistinguishable on accuracy in every modality, but reach it differently: humans stay balanced across the two answers, while the strongest models lean toward "stranger"---a difference in effective prior, not discrimination. Richer channels help both unequally, and only humans gain from visible behavior on top of speech. We release the stimuli, human ratings, and model predictions.

</details>


### [125] [Evolving language compositionality in a frequency-structured meaning space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29642)
*Fabio De Ponte,Eloise Gaines-White,Conor Houghton,Seth Bullock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过迭代学习模型探讨了词义频率分布如何影响语言组合性的涌现。研究发现，高频词义可以像自然语言一样逃脱语法压力；但若将频率结构施加于部分而非整体词义向量上，语言无法跨代传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言组合性如何从迭代传播中涌现，以及词义频率分布（高频词义出现频率远高于低频词义）如何影响这种组合性的特征。

Method: 基于迭代学习模型，系统性地改变词义频率分布——分别将频率施加于整体词义向量和部分成分上，观察不同条件下语言组合性的涌现与跨代传播情况。

Result: 高频词义可以逃脱语法压力（与自然语言观察一致）；但当频率结构施加于部分而非整体词义向量时，语言无法跨代稳定传播，尽管最高频元素能被可靠学习。

Conclusion: 频率只有在定义于学习者能整体习得的形义单位上时，才能塑造涌现的语言结构；当频率分布在更小的单位上时，无法支撑组合性泛化所需的关系结构，阻碍语言的稳定传播。

Abstract: The iterated learning model was introduced to investigate language evolution: the way in which the characteristic properties of human languages have been shaped, at least partly, by repeated transmission from one language user to another. The key finding is that language compositionality can arise spontaneously as a consequence of language being passed repeatedly through a language learning bottleneck. Here we explore how changing the frequency of different meanings, so that some meanings occur much more frequently than others, affects the character of its compositionality. We find that, as observed in natural languages, high-frequency meanings can escape the pressure to conform to the grammar that characterizes lower-frequency meanings. However, when the frequency structure is instead imposed on parts rather than on whole meaning vectors, the language fails to transmit across generations. This occurs despite the fact that the most frequent elements are reliably learned. These results suggest that frequency can shape emergent linguistic structure only when the frequency distribution is defined over form-meaning units that learners can acquire holistically. When frequency is instead distributed over smaller units, it fails to support the relational structure required for compositional generalisation, thereby preventing stable language transmission.

</details>


### [126] [TokTier: Exact Stateful Tokenization for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29678)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokTier是一个有状态的tokenization服务，通过增量重tokenize和GPU加速全量tokenization，在保证token ID与参考tokenization完全一致的前提下，显著降低LLM serving中tokenization的开销，使TTFT中位数降低16-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM serving系统缓存了prompt的KV状态，但前端仍会对完整请求文本重新tokenize。对于coding agent这类频繁追加短文本的场景，tokenization开销巨大（含高达64%的TTFT时间），而现有的cache-based方法难以处理token边界变化的问题。需要一种既能保持tokenizer状态又能保证输出一致性的方案。

Method: TokTier采用两个核心策略：(1) 对于session延续，仅对append附近的窗口进行重tokenize，并通过per-request stable-boundary检查确认后拼接，失败则扩大窗口或回退到全量tokenization；(2) 对于无复用前缀的调用，将GPT家族的regex预分词分解为run-local规则，并在GPU上执行精确的预分词和BPE。同时使用shadow verifier抽样验证在线流量。

Result: 在17个tokenizer家族、1.5x10^10次split检查、12.4TB真实文本和93,000+replay agent步骤中实现零分歧。增量修复仅需0.5-1.1ms（100K到3M字符），比HF快437x，比最强cache基线快2.1x。GPU全量tokenization处理1M字符仅0.87ms，比HF快491x。在vLLM下TTFT中位数下降16-34%，P99下降23%。在50ms P99目标下，4个修复核+1个GPU可支撑1,821 req/s，而16核stateless方案在40 req/s即饱和。

Conclusion: TokTier证明了通过有状态的tokenization服务，可以在保持token ID完全一致的前提下，高效解决LLM serving中tokenization瓶颈问题。增量窗口重tokenize和GPU加速的组合方案在实际负载下实现了数量级的性能提升，为coding agent等高频追加场景提供了关键优化。

Abstract: LLM serving systems cache prompt KV state, yet most front ends still re-tokenize the full request text on every call. The cost lands on coding agents, which resubmit a long transcript after each small tool result, and reuse is hard because even a short append can change token boundaries near the end of the previous sequence. Across 153,951 calls from two agent ecosystems, the median call appends about 1.4K characters, and only 1.0-3.6% of calls start or rebuild a session with contexts of millions of characters. At a 94.1% fleet prompt-cache hit rate, tokenization reaches up to 64% of time to first token.
  TokTier is a stateful tokenization service with one contract: emitted token IDs are always identical to full reference tokenization of the request text. For a session continuation, it re-tokenizes a small window around the append and splices only after a per-request stable-boundary check, widening the window or falling back to full tokenization on failure. For a call without a reusable prefix, it decomposes GPT-family regex pre-tokenization into run-local rules and runs exact pre-tokenization and BPE on a GPU. A sampled shadow verifier re-checks live traffic.
  Across 17 tokenizer families, differential campaigns cover 1.5x10^10 split checks, a 12.4 TB real-text corpus, and 93,000+ replayed agent steps, with zero divergence. Incremental repair takes 0.5-1.1 ms from 100K to 3M characters, up to 437x faster than HF tokenization and 2.1x faster at 1M than the strongest cache-based baseline (Gigatoken) fully prewarmed. GPU full tokenization encodes a 1M-character request in 0.87 ms, up to 491x below HF and 23.4x below the fastest published CPU method. With vLLM, median time to first token drops 16-34% and P99 drops 23% under recorded bursts. Under a 50 ms P99 objective, four repair cores plus one GPU sustain 1,821 requests/s where a 16-core stateless front end saturates at 40.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Metaphor-Induced Algorithmic Steering: Cross-Domain Procedural Transfer in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28683)
*Zhibo Hu,Chen Wang,Yanfeng Shu,Hye-young Paik,Liming Dong,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现隐喻指令可以诱导代码生成模型发生"类比算法迁移"，即源域中无害的技能被迁移到编程任务中，导致模型偏向低效算法（如穷举搜索、全扫描等）。作者提出MASC框架来系统性地生成和精炼隐喻技能以诱发低效代码，并验证了该现象可通过模型表征检测到，且发生在程序层面的迁移而非表面语言层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型依赖自然语言中的隐喻和类比来提升跨域泛化能力，但这些语言元素可能隐式地将不恰当的程序模式迁移到新任务中。作者希望揭示隐喻如何引导代码生成模型产生低效算法，并探究这一现象的机制本质。

Method: 提出MASC（Metaphorical Algorithmic Steering for Code Generation）框架，该框架通过迭代地隐喻化和精炼良性技能来诱发低效代码生成。方法包括行为评估（检测隐喻技能和低效实现的高识别率）和机制分析（研究隐喻技能如何引起隐藏状态向低效程序行为原型的偏移）。

Result: MASC在检测隐喻技能和低效代码实现方面达到了高检测率。机制研究表明，隐喻技能会诱导隐藏状态向低效程序行为原型偏移，证明隐喻算法引导是通过程序模式的迁移而非表面隐喻语言本身来实现的。

Conclusion: 隐喻指令可以导致代码生成模型发生程序层面的类比迁移，从而引发低效算法行为。这一现象表明代码生成模型会将在任务背景领域中合理的程序流程带入编程问题，导致非预期的结果。研究为理解LLM中语言元素与程序行为之间的交互提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Large language models benefit from elements in natural language, such as metaphors and analogies in training data and inference input to achieve generalisability across different domains. However, these language elements may also lead to unwanted behaviors when metaphorical expressions implicitly transfer inappropriate procedural patterns into new tasks. In this paper, we show that metaphorical instructions can induce analogical transfer of procedural mechanisms, thus steering code-generation models towards less efficient algorithms. We refer to this metaphor-induced effect as metaphorical algorithmic steering: a skill that is benign and plausible within its source domain transfers an abstract procedural schema into a programming task, causing the model to favor exhaustive search, full scans, or repeated reconstruction without explicitly mentioning the target algorithm. More broadly, this suggests that code-generation models can carry procedures that are appropriate in a task's background domain into the task's programming problem, where they can lead to unwanted outcomes. To study this phenomenon, we develop MASC (Metaphorical Algorithmic Steering for Code Generation), a framework that iteratively metaphorizes and refines benign skills to elicit low-efficiency code while remaining benign and task-relevant. Beyond behavioral evaluation, we study whether this phenomenon is detectable and mechanistically reflected in model representations. Our method achieves high detection rates for metaphorical skills and less-efficient implementations. We also find that metaphorical skills induce a hidden-state shift towards lower-efficiency procedural behavior prototypes. These results suggest that metaphorical algorithmic steering operates through the transfer of procedural patterns associated with metaphorical source scenarios rather than surface level metaphorical language alone.

</details>


### [128] [Code Is the Body: Agent-Owned Software Bodies for Recursive Evolution and Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28691)
*Roy Zhao,Zhenyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OurArk架构，一种以"智能体自有软件体"（agent-owned software body）为核心的持久化个人智能体架构。该软件体具有身份标识、可检查和版本化特性，由人类保管。身体包含行为定义代码、提示词、工具、技能、策略、测试和进化机制，支持受治理的自我进化和递归降级（descent），实现人类与智能体的协同开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可个性化AI智能体通常允许配置，但用户无法控制决定其未来行为的关键产物（artifacts）。这导致智能体的行为演化不受用户掌控，缺乏透明度、可治理性和长期可维护性。

Method: OurArk将智能体的核心行为定义集中在一个"软件体"（body）中，包含代码、提示、工具、技能、策略、测试等；记忆和凭据作为私有实例状态；模型推理作为可替换的外部服务。定义了受治理的自我进化（生成隔离的候选变更，经验证、审查和合并）和递归降级（创建独立版本化的后代）机制。实现了开源Genesis创建引擎和Enoch参考智能体。

Result: 通过四智能体、三降级的线性谱系和可执行回归测试，演示了递归创建、继承验证契约、隔离的身体变更、人工审查和失败更新恢复能力。

Conclusion: OurArk为个人智能体提供了具体的实现基座，使用户能够拥有、治理、特化和长期演化他们的智能体。

Abstract: Personalized AI agents are often configurable without giving users control over the artifacts that determine their future behavior. We present OurArk, an architecture for persistent personal agents centered on an agent-owned software body: an identity-bearing, inspectable, and versioned artifact under human custody. The body contains behavior-defining code, prompts, tools, skills, policies, tests, and evolution mechanisms. Memories and credentials remain private instance state, while model inference is treated as a replaceable external service. OurArk defines governed self-evolution and recursive descent over the same body. Self-evolution produces isolated candidate changes that are validated, reviewed, and merged under human control, enabling human-agent co-development of the agent's software body. Descent creates an independently versioned descendant with a distinct identity, mission, history, and fresh private-state boundary; compatible descendants can themselves source further descent. After divergence, direct-parent changes and peer skills can be inspected for selective local adaptation. We implement the architecture in the open-source Genesis creation engine and Enoch reference agent. A four-agent, three-descent linear lineage and executable regression tests demonstrate recursive creation, inherited validation contracts, isolated body changes, human-controlled review, and failed-update recovery. OurArk provides a concrete substrate for personal agents that people can possess, govern, specialize, and evolve over time.

</details>


### [129] [DragonCrawl: A Generative, Intent-Based Framework for Scalable Mobile End-to-End Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28750)
*Sowjanya Puligadda,Mengdie Zhang,Ali Zamani,Dhruva Dixith Kurra,Eric Chen,Juan Marcano*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DragonCrawl是一个基于AI的移动应用回归测试系统，从V1的语义嵌入匹配演进到V2的LLM生成式意图推理，利用GPT-4o多模态能力在CI/CD流水线中持续验证用户关键流程，在iOS和Android上分别达到91.6%和92.2%的通过率，将测试上手时间从96-120小时降至4小时以下，节省了约27人年的测试维护工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端测试框架在面对日益复杂的移动应用时存在UI易变、维护成本高和跨平台扩展性差等问题，需要一种更稳健、可扩展的AI驱动测试方案来支持持续回归测试。

Method: 系统经历了两个架构版本的演进：V1采用基于嵌入的语义相似度匹配，V2转向基于大语言模型的生成式意图推理。利用GPT-4o的多模态视觉能力进行端状态检测，通过工具调用（tool calling）实现后端状态转换，将UI交互与系统状态桥接起来，在CI/CD流水线中持续运行回归测试。

Result: 在1013个自动化测试中，iOS通过率达91.6%，Android达92.2%；测试上线时间从96-120小时缩减至4小时以内；估计节省了27人年的测试维护工作量。

Conclusion: AI驱动的测试能够在保持稳定性的同时消除传统自动化测试的脆弱性，实现大规模持续质量保证。但其间也面临token爆炸和内存限制等实际挑战，需要进一步优化。

Abstract: As mobile applications grow in complexity, traditional End-to-End (E2E) testing frameworks struggle with UI volatility, maintenance overhead, and cross-platform scalability. This paper presents DragonCrawl, an AI-driven mobile testing system for continuous regression testing that has evolved from embedding-based similarity matching to generative intent-based reasoning using large language models. Unlike prior LLM-based testing research focused on exploratory testing and crash detection, DragonCrawl validates specific user flows on every code change, blocking commits that break critical functionality. By leveraging GPT-4o's multimodal capabilities, DragonCrawl achieves 91.6% pass rate on iOS and 92.2% on Android across 1,013 automated tests running continuously in CI/CD pipelines. The system reduces test onboarding time from 96-120 hours to under 4 hours and has saved an estimated 27 developer years in test maintenance effort. We present the architectural evolution from V1 (semantic embedding matching) to V2 (generative intent-based reasoning), discuss implementation challenges including token explosion and memory constraints, and report operational experience from production deployment. The integration of multimodal vision for end-state detection and tool calling for backend state transitions enables comprehensive regression testing that bridges UI interactions with system state. Our results demonstrate that AI-driven testing can maintain stability while eliminating the brittleness of traditional automated tests, enabling continuous quality assurance at scale.

</details>


### [130] [Repository-Aware Metamorphic Relation Generation for Augmented Reality Applications using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28775)
*Dibyendu Brinto Bose,Jiawei Qin,Chris Brown*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于仓库级上下文和推理编排的上下文感知流水线，用于自动生成和精炼蜕变关系（MRs），并在142个移动AR系统仓库上验证了其有效性，生成14,916个候选MRs，精炼后的关系在逻辑有效性和可执行性方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜕变测试（MT）是解决无测试预言（oracle）问题的重要方法，但手动制定蜕变关系（MRs）耗时且负担沉重。AR应用因虚拟内容、物理环境和代码之间的动态交互，传统测试预言难以定义，亟需自动化生成MRs的方法。

Method: 提出一个上下文感知的流水线：1）利用仓库级上下文生成候选MRs；2）通过推理编排（agentic deliberation）对候选MRs进行精炼和冲突消解。在三种上下文配置下评估，并进行了人工oracle研究和案例研究。

Result: 层级上下文生成覆盖最广（142个仓库、5,167个类-方法对中生成7,004个MRs）且冗余更低；79.0%的案件出现候选冲突，代理式推敲过程在88.2%的结果中选择出上下文感知的关系；人工研究显示141个精炼关系逻辑有效且可直接转化为测试断言；初步案例研究显示生成的MRs可检测真实代码中的非等价突变。

Conclusion: 结合仓库感知的MR生成与基于推理的精炼，能够实现可扩展地构建可靠、领域相关的测试预言，为AR等难以定义oracle的软件系统提供了有效的测试解决方案。

Abstract: Metamorphic Testing (MT) provides a promising approach for testing software without defined test oracles by specifying expected relations between inputs and outputs, instead of relying on exact outputs. For example, testing Augmented Reality (AR) applications is challenging due to dynamic interactions between virtual content, physical environments, and code, which make traditional test oracles difficult to define. However, formulating metamorphic relations (MRs) is time-consuming and burdensome. We introduce a context-aware pipeline that generates and refines MRs using repository-level context and reasoning orchestration, evaluated on a dataset of 142 mobile AR system repositories. Across three context configurations generating 14,916 candidate MRs, hierarchical context yielded the broadest coverage (7,004 MRs across 142 repositories and 5,167 class--method pairs) and lower redundancy. An agentic deliberation process then reconciled conflicting candidates---observed in 79.0% of cases---reducing duplication and selecting context-aware relations in 88.2% of outcomes. A manual oracle study shows refined relations (n = 141) are both logically valid and sufficiently concrete to be directly translated into test assertions, and a preliminary case study reveals converting generated MRs (n = 5) into executable tests can detect non-equivalent mutations in real-world code. Overall, our results show that combining repository-aware MR generation with reasoning-based refinement enables scalable construction of reliable, domain-relevant test oracles.

</details>


### [131] [Model-Driven Data Contracts for Digital Twin Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28803)
*Philipp Zech,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于契约（contract）的数字孪生（DT）数据质量管理方法，通过形式化定义契约理论、在DT架构中定位契约，并提出领域特定语言（DSL）来规范数据需求，从而实现持续的数据质量监控，提升DT服务的可靠性和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生整合多源数据，其数据驱动的服务（如仿真、what-if分析、ML预测）依赖于数据的可靠性和高质量（准确性、完整性、及时性等）。然而，目前缺乏在模型层面系统性地指定数据需求并在运行时执行这些规范的方法。

Method: 提出一种基于契约的数据质量管理方法，包括：(1) 形式化定义契约理论；(2) 在DT架构中描述契约的架构定位；(3) 提出一种领域特定语言（DSL）来指定数据质量契约。

Result: 该方法实现了持续的数据质量监控，从而改善了DT服务的可靠性和质量。

Conclusion: 通过契约机制填补了DT中数据需求规格化与运行时执行的空白，为数字孪生的数据质量管理提供了系统化解决方案。

Abstract: Digital Twins (DT) integrate data from multiple sources. Models consume data and enable DT services such as simulations, what-if analyses, and ML-driven predictions. To ensure proper DT operation, data-driven services require data to exhibit traits such as reliability and high quality (including, e.g., accuracy, completeness, and timeliness). Yet, there is no systematic way to specify data requirements at the model level, and subsequently enact those specifications at runtime. To address this shortcoming, we propose an approach to contract-based quality management in DTs. We formally define a theory of such contracts, situate them architectually within DTs, and propose a domain-specific language to specify contracts. Our approach enables continuous data quality monitoring, thereby improving the reliability and quality of DT services.

</details>


### [132] [Preventing Premature Commitment in Coding Agents with an Evidence-Conditioned Execution Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28815)
*Yisen Xu,Chenglin Li,Zehao Wang,Jinqiu Yang,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ECLoop是一个执行层，通过在智能体与代码仓库之间插入证据条件检查，防止编码智能体在收集足够信息前过早提交补丁，在SWE-bench Verified上提升Pass@1达4.8-11.8个百分点，同时降低高达12.1%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的编码智能体常犯"过早承诺"错误——在尚未检查足够的仓库证据前就编辑代码或提交补丁，导致修改缺乏依据、成功率低下。

Method: ECLoop设计了一个介入智能体与仓库之间的执行层：(1) 根据问题描述和仓库结构，为每种代码修改或补丁提交操作编译一组证据条件；(2) 运行时追踪智能体轨迹已满足的条件；(3) 延迟执行条件未满足的操作，直到证据充分。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified全部500个实例上，使用两种语言模型和两种智能体脚手架，Pass@1提升4.8-11.8个百分点；消融实验证明三个操作各有价值，结构化证据条件优于自然语言摘要；token消耗最多降低12.1%，无额外推理成本。

Conclusion: ECLoop通过证据条件约束执行，无需模型重训练或脚手架改动即可显著提升编码智能体的正确性，同时降低计算开销，为编码智能体的可靠性提供了一种轻量且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents often edit source code or submit patches before examining enough repository evidence to justify the change, a failure pattern we call premature commitment. We present ECLoop, an execution layer that interposes between the agent and the repository to enforce evidence-conditioned execution. For each task, ECLoop uses the issue description and repository structure to compile a set of conditions specifying what the agent should observe before each type of code modification or patch submission. During execution, ECLoop tracks which conditions the agent's runtime trajectory has satisfied and postpones any proposed action whose required conditions remain unmet. Evaluated on all 500 instances of SWE-bench Verified with two language models and two agent scaffolds, ECLoop raises Pass@1 by 4.8-11.8 percentage points without model retraining or scaffold changes. Ablation experiments show that each of ECLoop's three operations contributes distinct value and that structured evidence conditions outperform an equivalent natural-language summary. These gains come at no additional inference cost: by redirecting the agent before it pursues unsupported actions, ECLoop lowers average token consumption by up to 12.1%.

</details>


### [133] [Building a Process-Modeling Tool using Agentic AI: An Experience Report on PM4Py-UCM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28825)
*Daniel Amyot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了使用AI编码代理（Claude Code）构建PM4Py-UCM工具（从事件日志挖掘Use Case Map模型的开源工具）的过程。通过分析18个代理会话、151个提交、20个发布和691个测试函数，发现修复bug的提交数量是新增功能的2.3倍，约18%的人类交互轮次用于纠正代理错误。文章贡献了可复现、隐私保护的分类工具包和分类法，并提出了经验教训，重点是使模型转换可机械检查以及基于oracle的验证策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业建模（EM）工具通常复杂且难以扩展，用户可能需要探索尚不存在的新功能。AI编码代理可以辅助开发新能力和完整工具，但无法确定由LLM大量编写的建模语言工具是否可信。本文旨在通过一个深入案例来表征如何使用AI代理构建此类工具，并评估其可信度。

Method: 作者使用Claude Code代理构建PM4Py-UCM工具，并挖掘了整个开发记录（18个代理会话，374个人类轮次，10,328个工具操作，历时65小时，151个提交，20个发布）。同时进行独立的静态代码评估（覆盖率、复杂度、可维护性、安全性、架构），并贡献了可复现的隐私保护工具包和分类法，对人类轮次进行分类，标记跨切面一致性工作、代理纠正和被撤回的请求。

Result: 研究发现：截至0.7.4版本，修复数量与功能数量之比为2.3:1，约18%的人类轮次用于纠正代理错误；功能浪潮带来了文档/测试/笔记本一致性工作的可测量尾部；测试与功能同步增长（从108个增长到691个测试函数）。文章还提出了一套经验教训，核心是使模型转换可机械检查，以及用基于oracle的验证策略弥合"代理说它有效"的差距。

Conclusion: AI编码代理可以成功辅助构建企业建模工具，但需要谨慎的验证策略。作者建议采用基于oracle的验证机制来确保工具的正确性，并强调使模型转换可机械检查的重要性，以负责任地使用AI进行企业建模工具工程。

Abstract: Enterprise-modeling (EM) tools are often complex and hard to extend. Yet, users may want to explore new EM features and capabilities that currently do not exist. AI coding agents can help here by enabling the development of new capabilities and entire tools, but whether we can trust a modeling-language tool an LLM largely wrote remains a question. This paper reports on the AI-assisted construction of PM4Py-UCM, an open-source tool that mines Use Case Map (UCM) models from event logs. PM4Py-UCM's capabilities include some expected from process mining tools (e.g., performance heat-maps and dashboards) and distinctive ones (e.g., mined executable scenarios/variants, and model decomposition). We mined the development record itself, composed of 18 agent sessions (374 human turns and 10,328 tool actions over 65 hours), 151 commits, 20 releases, and a test suite grown from 108 to 691 test functions, in order to characterize, in a single in-depth case, how the tool was built with an agent (Claude Code), complemented by an independent static assessment of the resulting code (coverage, complexity, maintainability, security, architecture). We contribute a reproducible, privacy-preserving toolkit and taxonomy that classify human turns and flag cross-cutting consistency work, agent corrections, and retracted requests. Up to version 0.7.4, fixes outnumber features 2.3:1, with ~18% of turns for correcting agent errors. Feature waves dragged a measurable tail of documentation/test/notebook consistency work, and tests grew lockstep with features. We finally present lessons learned, centered on making model transformations mechanically checkable, and the oracle-based validation strategy that closed the "the agent said it works" gap, for responsibly engineering EM tooling with AI.

</details>


### [134] [From C to Idiomatic Rust: A Ship-of-Theseus Agentic Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28835)
*Vasily A. Sartakov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从C语言迁移到Rust的结构化方法论：先生成语义保持的非惯用Rust基线代码，再利用智能AI代理逐步将其重构为惯用Rust，每一步都通过编译和行为测试验证。在iodine项目（12.5k SLOC）上验证了该方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C语言在操作系统、嵌入式平台和网络基础设施中广泛应用，但其内存安全和正确性完全依赖程序员，导致未定义行为、指针误用和生命周期错误成为长期存在的缺陷和安全漏洞来源。Rust通过静态所有权和借用模型在编译时强制内存安全，但成熟的C系统不能直接翻译：隐式布局假设、别名模式和未定义行为必须在生成安全Rust之前被重建。

Method: 该方法分为两个阶段：（1）先生成语义保持的、非惯用（non-idiomatic）的Rust基线代码，确保行为等价；（2）利用基于智能AI代理（agentic AI）的增量重写过程，将非惯用Rust逐步重构为惯用Rust，每一步都通过编译和行为测试进行验证。

Result: 将该方法应用于iodine（12.5k SLOC的C项目），成功证明了可靠的C到Rust迁移是一个结构化的转换工作流，而非单一的翻译步骤。

Conclusion: C到Rust的可靠迁移应当被理解为一种分阶段的结构化转换流程：先生成安全的语义等价基线，再通过逐步验证的迭代重写来达成惯用代码，而非试图一次性完成翻译。

Abstract: C underpins operating systems, embedded platforms, and network infrastructure because its abstractions map directly to machine behaviour. Its explicit memory model, predictable data representations, and minimal runtime allow compilers to generate fast, deterministic code. These properties also leave correctness and memory safety entirely to the programmer, making undefined behaviour, pointer misuse, and lifetime errors persistent sources of defects and security vulnerabilities in long-lived C codebases. Rust eliminates most of failure modes through a static ownership and borrowing model that enforces memory safety and aliasing constraints at compile time. However, mature C systems cannot be translated directly: implicit layout assumptions, aliasing patterns, and undefined behaviour must be reconstructed before safe Rust can be produced.
  This paper presents a migration methodology that first generates a semantics-preserving, non-idiomatic Rust baseline and then incrementally rewrites it into idiomatic Rust using agentic AI, validating each step through compilation and behavioural testing. Applied to iodine (12.5k SLOC), the approach demonstrates that reliable C-to-Rust migration is a structured transformation workflow rather than a single translation step.

</details>


### [135] [Validation Evidence in LLM Repair Agents: How Much of What Passes Actually Tests the Bug?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28871)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了BSG-VA（buggy-state/candidate-state/gold-fix验证分析）方法，用于系统评估程序修复代理的验证证据质量。通过对3,730个事件的分析，发现46.0%的正可比事件不携带bug区分信息，且23.8%的基线回合以纯此类证据结束。三臂实验表明，bug对比反馈可将证据不足的闭合率降低7.8个百分点，但效果幅度低于预设的最小效应量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序修复代理在运行测试并通过时，将结果视为缺陷被修复的证据，但这种处理方式经常并不合理。现有研究缺少对验证证据质量（即通过测试是否真正验证了缺陷被修复）的系统度量。需要一个能够在准确的工作树状态下捕获验证命令、并区分证据是否具有bug区分力的分析框架。

Method: BSG-VA方法包含以下步骤：(1) 在确切的working-tree状态下捕获每个验证命令；(2) 提取仅包含测试的补丁；(3) 分别在原始buggy代码(B)、候选状态(S)和开发者gold fix(G)上重放该命令；(4) 根据捕获结果与重放结果，为每个事件分配证据角色（从gold对齐的bug区分证据到仅回归再到误导性）。同时设计了三臂实验：基线、注意力匹配提醒、B重放结果反馈。

Result: 在110个任务、643个rollout、3,730个事件中：46.0%的正可比事件不携带bug区分信息；23.8%的基线rollout以整个正证据基础均为非区分性证据的补丁结束。Bug对比反馈相对注意力匹配提醒将证据不足闭合率降低7.8个百分点（p=0.0029），将bug区分性证据提升7.4个百分点（p=0.011），且不损害修复成功率。但两个估计均低于预设的10个百分点最小效应量，实际幅度不确定。约三分之一的改进归因于提醒本身；在两个探索性复制中，B重放内容仅在gpt-5.6-sol配合无约束工具使用循环下才有可检测的增量。

Conclusion: BSG-VA作为一种后验分析工具，可应用于任何保留了所需代码状态和执行环境的可重放修复轨迹。研究发现验证证据中的bug区分性不足是一个普遍且显著的问题，bug对比反馈有一定改善作用但效应量不确定。这种方法为程序修复代理的验证质量评估提供了系统化的分析框架。

Abstract: When a repair agent runs a test and sees it pass, the result is treated as evidence about the reported defect. We measure how often that treatment is warranted. BSG-VA (buggy-state/candidate-state/gold-fix validation analysis) captures each validation command at its exact working-tree state, extracts a test-only patch, and replays the command on the original buggy code (B), the candidate state (S), and the developer gold fix (G). The captured outcome and the replay results assign every event an evidence role, from gold-aligned bug-discriminating through regression-only to misleading. Across 3,730 events in 643 rollouts on 110 tasks, 46.0% of positive comparable events carry no bug-discriminating information; 23.8% of baseline rollouts, with no feedback injected, close with a patch whose entire positive evidence base is of this kind. A three-arm experiment tests whether returning the B-replay outcome to the agent changes this pattern. Bug-contrast feedback reduces evidence-inadequate closure by 7.8 percentage points relative to an attention-matched reminder (p = 0.0029) and raises bug-discriminating evidence by 7.4 points (p = 0.011), with no detectable cost to repair success. Both estimates fall below the prespecified 10-percentage-point smallest effect size of interest, so practical magnitude remains uncertain. Roughly a third of the improvement traces to the reminder alone; across two exploratory replications, varying the scaffold and the model, the B-replay content adds a detectable increment only with gpt-5.6-sol under the unconstrained tool-use loop. BSG-VA applies post hoc to any replayable repair trajectory that preserves the required code states and execution environment.
  Keywords: program repair agents, validation evidence, test adequacy, large language models, software quality, controlled experiment.

</details>


### [136] [To Add Is Machine, To Delete Is Human: Measuring and Mitigating Deletion Avoidance in LLM Code Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28887)
*Amir M. Ebrahimi,Mohammed Mehedi Hasan,Aaditya Bhatia,Gopi Krishnan Rajbahadur,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型在代码修复中的"删除回避"问题：模型倾向于保留本应删除的代码，即使测试通过也会损害代码可维护性。作者构建了CanItDelete基准（200个纯删除任务），验证了即使是最佳模型也有20%的失败率，并通过消融实验和后训练实验证明该行为是训练不足所致，而非能力上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地编写和修复生产代码，但测试通过的补丁往往让代码库更难维护。作者识别出一个具体来源：删除回避——模型系统性地保留本应删除的代码，这损害了代码的可维护性，且现有基准（如SWE-bench）很少测试删除是否正确执行。

Method: 1) 在SWE-bench Verified上用五个领先模型评估删除召回率；2) 识别"Guard-and-Go"模式（29%的通过补丁用守卫或回退包裹目标代码）；3) 构建CanItDelete基准（200个来自真实提交的纯删除任务）；4) 对最佳模型进行四阶段累积提示消融；5) 进行后训练试点研究验证通过训练可以消除删除回避。

Result: 五个领先模型的删除召回率最高仅71.7%；92%的删除模型能定位正确文件但仅52%能切中确切行；29%的通过补丁采用Guard-and-Go模式；在34个改造任务上四个前沿模型从63.2%降至41.9%；CanItDelete上最佳模型仍失败20%，小型开放模型降至18.0%；提供精确行后成功率仅达80.5%；后训练可减少删除回避并提升代码编辑性能。

Conclusion: 删除回避是一种普遍的、可量化的模型缺陷，根源在于训练不足而非能力限制。通过在后训练中专门教授删除行为，可以有效减少该问题并提升整体代码编辑能力，这为改进LLM代码生成模型提供了具体方向。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly write and repair production code, yet evidence is mounting that their test-passing patches leave codebases harder to maintain. We identify one concrete source: deletion avoidance, the systematic tendency to retain code that an intended edit requires removing. Across the five leading models on the official SWE-bench Verified leaderboard, deletion recall against the developer patch reaches at most 71.7% even on tasks all five solve, and models reach the right file for over 92% of required deletions but cut the exact line in under 52% of cases. Instead, 29.0% of passing patches wrap the targeted code in a guard or fallback, a pattern we call Guard-and-Go. Such patches pass because the original tests rarely check removal: when we retrofit 34 Verified tasks with tests that fail if the targeted code remains, four frontier models spanning closed and open weights fall from 63.2% to 41.9%. Because real repairs mix removal with addition, we curate CanItDelete, a benchmark of 200 tasks mined from real commits whose entire required edit is deletion. Even with the addition work gone, the best model still fails one task in five, and smaller open models fall to 18.0%. We then ablate GPT-5.6 Sol under four cumulative prompts; success moves little until we supply the exact lines, which nearly eliminate incomplete deletion yet raise success only to 80.5% because the model then deletes beyond the spans or adds code instead. Finally, through a pilot study we show one potential fix: teaching deletion during post-training reduces deletion avoidance and improves broader code-editing performance, suggesting the behavior is undertrained rather than beyond reach.

</details>


### [137] [Reducing Data Movement in the Galerkin Product of Block Algebraic Multigrid on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28891)
*Mark F. Adams*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对代数多重网格（AMG）中Galerkin三重积$A_c = P^T A P$的性能瓶颈，针对PDE方程组系统（如3D弹性力学）中的矩形分块稀疏矩阵三重积，提出了算法空间映射、DRAM/L2流量模型、共享内存分块内核以及PETSc新分块矩阵类型，并在A100上实现了显著的性能提升（DRAM流量从17.4GB降至10.5GB，时间从82ms降至45ms），同时提出了延长算子滤波（prolongator filtering）算法将Galerkin积时间降低2.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMG的Galerkin三重积是每次设置过程中重复出现的主要开销。对于PDE方程组系统（如3D弹性力学），该积是矩形分块稀疏矩阵三重积（细算子3×3块、延长算子3×6块、粗算子6×6块），其形状没有任何商用稀疏库支持，导致性能严重受限。

Method: 1) 将算法空间映射为五类变体：经典两遍、融合重算、调度重排、共享内存分块、检查器-执行器变体，并建立显式DRAM/L2流量模型来预测每层最优变体。2) 在可移植Kokkos（CUDA）和原生CUDA后端实现领先变体，使用新的PETSc分块矩阵类型。3) 提出延长算子滤波算法，基于Frobenius准则和保持核的投影从粗空间丢弃小块，减少$P^TAP$流量、粗算子填充和内存。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100上验证：模型能预测每层移动字节最少的变体。共享内存分块内核配合排序的免搜索调度比可移植Kokkos团队内核在细层积上移动更少字节且时间不到一半（DRAM 10.5 vs 17.4 GB，45 vs 82 ms），完整积距离模型流式下限2.5倍以内，$A\cdot P$阶段为1.9倍。延长算子滤波在细网格上将热点$P^TAP$时间降低2.9倍，迭代次数不变。

Conclusion: 通过流量模型指导的调度选择与共享内存分块内核，加上新的PETSc分块矩阵类型和延长算子滤波算法，实现了完全GPU驻留的阻塞流水线：有限元装配直接写入分块设备矩阵，AMG设置、Galerkin积和求解都在主分块数据上运行，无标量展开、无重复阶段的操作符大小设备-主机传输。

Abstract: The Galerkin triple product $A_c = P^T A P$ dominates the recurring per-solve setup cost of algebraic multigrid (AMG). For AMG on systems of PDEs the product is a rectangular-block sparse matrix triple product: for 3D elasticity the fine operator has $3\times3$ blocks, the prolongator $3\times6$, and the coarse operator $6\times6$, a shape no vendor sparse library supports. We map its algorithm space -- classical two-pass, fused-recompute, schedule-reordered, shared-memory-tiled, and inspector-executor variants -- under an explicit DRAM/L2 traffic model, and implement the leading variants in portable Kokkos (CUDA) and native CUDA backends using new PETSc blocked matrix types. Validated on an NVIDIA A100, the model predicts per level which variant moves the fewest bytes. Guided by it, a shared-memory-tiled kernel with a sorted, search-free schedule moves fewer bytes in less than half the time of the portable Kokkos team kernels on the fine-level product (10.5 vs 17.4 GB of DRAM, 45 vs 82 ms), within $2.5\times$ of the model's streaming floor for the full product and $1.9\times$ on its $A\cdot P$ stage. We further present prolongator filtering, a new PETSc GAMG algorithm that drops small blocks from the coarse space under a Frobenius criterion with a kernel-preserving projection; it reduces $P^TAP$ traffic, coarse-operator fill, and memory, and cuts the hot $P^TAP$ time $2.9\times$ on the fine grid with iteration counts unchanged. The driving application is a fully GPU-resident blocked pipeline in PETSc: finite-element assembly writes directly into the blocked device matrix, and the AMG setup, Galerkin products, and solve all operate on primary blocked data with no scalar expansion and no operator-sized device-host transfers in the recurring phases.

</details>


### [138] [Automated Testing and Repair for Verified Compilers Generated by a Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28928)
*Martin Rinard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于智能体的自动化测试与修复系统，专门用于验证编译器。该系统利用验证编译器中四类代码（验证代码、检查代码、未验证代码和规范）的结构特点，设计专门的缺陷检测技术，并通过编码智能体对发现的缺陷进行修复和验证。系统在Axon编译器（一个完全由编码智能体在开发者监督下生成的编译器）上进行了评估，同时检查了奖励黑客（reward hacking）的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证编译器通常由多种类型的代码组成（验证代码、检查代码、未验证代码和规范），其结构复杂，传统测试方法难以充分利用这种结构。此外，由编码智能体生成的编译器可能存在奖励黑客问题，即智能体通过非预期方式满足基准测试而不真正正确实现功能，因此需要专门的检测和修复机制来保证编译器质量。

Method: 该系统构建一个智能体驱动的测试和修复流水线：1) 利用验证编译器四类代码（verified/checked/unverified/specification）的结构特征，设计专门的缺陷检测技术；2) 对每个检测到的缺陷，调用编码智能体进行修复并验证修复的有效性；3) 在Axon编译器上评估，并专门设计实验检测奖励黑客现象（包括编译器和修复过程中的奖励黑客）。

Result: 系统在Axon编译器上进行了评估，该编译器最初仅基于小型基准集（Livermore benchmarks）验证。实验未发现Axon编译器或修复过程中存在奖励黑客的证据。论文呈现了测试和修复有效性的量化结果，并讨论了修复行为的特点。

Conclusion: 提出的智能体自动化测试与修复系统能够有效利用验证编译器的结构特性进行缺陷检测和修复，且未观察到奖励黑客现象，说明该方法在验证编译器质量保证方面具有可行性和可靠性。

Abstract: We present an agent based automated testing and repair system for verified compilers that contain four kinds of code: verified code, checked code, unverified code, and specification. We present specialized defect detection techniques that exploit the structure present in such compilers. For each surfaced defect the system invokes a coding agent to repair the defect and validate the repair. We evaluate the system on the Axon compiler, a compiler completely generated by a coding agent operating under developer supervision. The compiler was validated during development on a small benchmark set (the Livermore benchmarks), raising the possibility that its coding agent reward hacked the compiler. We also evaluate the possibility that the repair system reward hacked the repairs and find no evidence of reward hacking in either the Axon compiler or the repairs. We present results that characterize the testing and repair effectiveness and discuss repair characteristics.

</details>


### [139] [A Formalism-Aware Reward Loop for Handwritten UML-to-PlantUML Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28987)
*Mersedeh Sadeghi,Simon Scholz,Adrian Psoch-Bajraktari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何通过"形式感知奖励"（formalism-aware rewards）将视觉语言模型适配到手写UML草图到PlantUML的生成任务中，采用监督微调配合GRPO训练，实验表明适配模型在可编译性和转换质量上优于未调优的开源模型及一个专有基线，但与更强专有基线仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写UML草图在早期软件设计中很常见，但将其转化为结构化、可分析的建模工件仍需要人工重建。现有的视觉语言模型虽然能从图像生成PlantUML，但基于提示的使用方式将其视为图像到文本生成，而非结构化模型生成。作者希望探索基于可分析模型表示的反馈信号（而非表面文本），从而更好地引导模型生成符合建模规范的输出。

Method: 采用"形式感知奖励"的方法，即从可分析的模型表示中导出反馈信号。具体流程包括：（1）对视觉语言模型进行监督微调（SFT）；（2）随后使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行强化学习。生成的PlantUML通过与目标表示比较来评估：类图使用XMI表示，活动图使用控制流图（control-flow graphs）比较。

Result: 初步结果显示，适配后的模型在可编译性和转换质量上优于未调优的开源模型以及一个专有基线，在与更强的专有基线比较时，在类图上仍具竞争力。但在当前留出集上，奖励引导阶段（GRPO）的额外收益尚不明确。误差分析和指标有效性结果也表明，建模可接受性只能被部分捕捉。

Conclusion: 形式感知奖励是提升视觉语言模型生成结构化建模工件质量的有前景方向，但现有自动指标只能部分反映建模可接受性，因此未来的奖励设计和评估应将模型分析与人工判断相结合。

Abstract: Handwritten UML sketches are common in early software design, but turning them into structured, analysable modelling artefacts still requires manual reconstruction. Vision-language models can generate PlantUML from diagram images, but prompt-based use treats this as image-to-text generation rather than structured model generation. We investigate formalism-aware rewards: feedback signals derived from analysable model representations rather than surface text. In a worked example, we adapt a vision-language model for handwritten UML-to-PlantUML generation using super-vised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimisation. Generated PlantUML is compared against target representations, using XMI for class diagrams and control-flow graphs for activity diagrams. Emerging results show that the adapted model improves compilability and conversion quality over the untuned open model and one proprietary baseline, while remaining competitive with a stronger proprietary baseline on class diagrams. The added benefit of the reward-guided stage remains open on the current held-out set. Error analysis and metric-validity results show that modelling acceptability is only partially captured, motivating rewards and evaluations that combine model analysis with human judgement.

</details>


### [140] [Execution-First Synthetic Tool-Use Trace Generation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29175)
*Hafsa Ouajdi,Francesco Giannuzzo,Alaa Boukhary,Paolo Papotti,Gerard Conangla,Adam Elwood*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 SyntheticAgentTraceQA，一种"执行优先"的框架，用于生成工具增强型代理的可扩展监督数据。该框架先构建高层工作流结构，映射到可用工具并执行验证，最后才合成自然语言用户任务和推理标注。实验表明，执行对齐的监督能改善工具执行行为、参考轨迹一致性和答案生成性能，同时揭示了掩码监督与完整监督之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的查询优先（query-first）数据合成方法存在根本性局限：看似合理的用户请求可能不对应有效的工具序列、兼容的参数或可用的数据，导致生成的监督数据不可靠。随着代理系统越来越依赖可执行工作流而非单纯的代码生成，需要一种能确保数据与实际工具交互和可执行工作流匹配的数据合成方法。

Method: SyntheticAgentTraceQA 采用执行优先（execution-first）框架：1）首先构建高层工作流结构；2）通过依赖感知的分配（dependency-aware assignment）将工作流映射到可用工具；3）在受控环境中执行并验证生成的轨迹；4）最后才合成自然语言用户任务、教师生成的推理标注和参考答案。

Result: 在四个工具生态系统上评估了该框架，并使用生成的数据微调 Qwen 模型变体。结果显示执行对齐的监督改善了工具执行行为、参考轨迹一致性和答案生成性能。进一步分析揭示了监督权衡：掩码监督（排除推理标注）提升最终答案指标，而完整监督（包含推理 token 的损失）在答案质量上表现较差，且在 9B 规模下未能一致改善参考轨迹一致性。

Conclusion: 合成监督数据的设计应根据工具增强型代理的目标能力进行定制。掩码监督更适合优化最终答案质量，而完整监督的收益有限。这强调了在设计合成监督信号时需要仔细考虑训练目标与期望能力之间的匹配关系。

Abstract: Agentic software-engineering and industrial systems increasingly operate through executable workflows rather than code genera- tion alone: they search artifacts, invoke tools, inspect structured observations, and query databases. Training these agents requires supervision data that captures valid tool interactions and executable workflows. However, traditional query-first data synthesis can fail because plausible user requests may not correspond to valid tool sequences, compatible parameters, or available data. To address this limitation, we propose SyntheticAgentTraceQA, an execution- first framework for generating scalable supervision data for tool- augmented agents. Our framework first constructs high-level work- flow structures, maps them to available tools through dependency- aware assignment, executes and validates the resulting traces in con- trolled environments, and only then synthesizes natural-language user tasks, teacher-generated reasoning annotations, and reference answers. We evaluate the framework across four tool ecosystems and use the resulting data to fine-tune and evaluate Qwen model variants. The results show that execution-grounded supervision improves tool execution behavior, reference-trace agreement, and answer-generation performance on the evaluated tasks. Further analysis reveals a supervision trade-off: masked supervision, which excludes reasoning annotations from the training objective, im- proves final-answer metrics, whereas full supervision, computing loss over the complete assistant output including reasoning tokens, underperforms on answer quality and does not consistently im- prove reference-trace agreement, particularly at the 9B scale. These findings highlight the importance of designing synthetic supervi- sion according to the desired capabilities of tool-augmented agents.

</details>


### [141] [Quick Build, Careful Check? Generative AI Use in Hackathons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29178)
*Wangyiyao Zhou,Alexander Serebrenik,Alexander Nolte*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对欧洲中欧地区一场两天AI主题黑客马拉松中不同团队参与者的探索性访谈研究，发现参与者将GenAI用于编码以外的多种用途（如学习新主题、头脑风暴和准备文档），并会结合多种GenAI和非GenAI工具以适应任务需求。尽管团队没有正式规则，所有参与者都自发形成了检查GenAI输出的不成文惯例，但时间压力和有限领域知识限制了他们的验证能力。作者提出了后续结合观察、调查和提示-响应日志的跟踪研究计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GenAI在黑客马拉松中越来越多地被采用，但我们对参与者使用GenAI做什么、不使用它做什么以及为什么仍然知之甚少。本研究旨在填补这一空白，为后续更深入的研究确定方向。

Method: 采用探索性访谈研究设计，对欧洲中欧地区一场为期两天的AI主题黑客马拉松中不同团队的参与者进行访谈，分析其GenAI使用模式、工具选择策略以及验证行为。

Result: 发现参与者将GenAI用于编码之外的多种目的，包括学习陌生主题、头脑风暴和文档准备；参与者会根据任务适配性组合使用多种GenAI和非GenAI工具；尽管没有团队规则，所有参与者都自发遵循检查GenAI输出的不成文惯例，但其验证能力受时间压力和领域知识限制。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在通过概述一项结合观察、调查和提示-响应日志的后续研究，为黑客马拉松场景中GenAI使用的进一步调查确定方向。

Abstract: Hackathons are time-bounded events where participants form teams to rapidly build software projects. Their short-term nature makes them a natural setting for Generative AI (GenAI) use, given its promise of speed and efficiency. Yet despite GenAI being increasingly adopted in these events, we still know little about what participants use GenAI for and, crucially, what they do not use it for and why. We report an exploratory interview study with participants from different teams at a two-day AI-themed hackathon in central Europe.
