We found that participants used GenAI for purposes beyond coding, including learning unfamiliar topics, brainstorming, and preparing documentation. At the same time, they combined multiple GenAI and non-GenAI tools depending on task fit. We also found that, despite the absence of team rules, all participants we studied converged on an unwritten practice of checking GenAI output before using it, yet their ability to actually verify was constrained by time pressure and limited domain knowledge. This work aims to identify avenues for further investigation by outlining a follow-up study combining observation, surveys, and prompt-and-response logs.

</details>


### [142] [Metamorphic Testing of Transpilers via Mutation Consistency of Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29247)
*Enea Raffaele Ilario Papaleo,Luca Guglielmo,Giovanni Denaro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对转译器（transpiler）的新型蜕变测试技术 MCP-Tester。与传统的基于运行时行为的测试方法不同，该方法直接在转译器生成的源代码上定义蜕变关系，利用"变异一致性"（mutation consistency）属性来检测故障。案例研究表明该方法能有效发现纯模糊测试无法检测的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转译器在工业领域（特别是依赖DSL的领域）中广泛使用，确保其正确性至关重要。然而现有编译器测试方法难以推广到转译器：差分测试因缺少多个等价实现而受阻；基于蜕变测试的方法假设能执行编译后的二进制文件，但转译器输出的是源代码，需要复杂工具链和硬件环境，这一假设常常不成立。

Method: 提出一种针对转译器的蜕变测试技术。其核心思想是在转译器生成的源代码上直接定义蜕变关系，而非依赖运行时行为。这些关系捕捉"变异一致性"属性：对输入DSL程序进行变异风格的修改，应当在生成的输出中引发可预测且结构一致的改变。作者将该方法实现为工具MCP-Tester。

Result: 通过在一个技术转移项目的案例研究中评估MCP-Tester，实验结果表明该方法能有效揭示纯模糊测试（pure fuzzing）无法检测到的故障。

Conclusion: 针对转译器特有挑战（无法执行输出代码）提出了一种直接在源代码层面进行蜕变测试的新方法，在工业案例中验证了其有效性，为转译器测试提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Transpilers are increasingly used for software development, especially in industrial domains that rely on domain-specific languages (DSLs), to allow engineers to work with familiar concepts and appropriate abstractions. Ensuring the correctness of these instruments is therefore critical in many industrial settings. This paper observes that existing approaches for compiler testing hardly generalize to transpilers. Differential testing approaches are hindered as multiple equivalent implementations of the transpiler under test are seldom available in practice. The approaches based on metamorphic testing assume the ability to execute the compiled binaries, an assumption that cannot be always made for transpilers, which oftentimes produce results expressed as source code, requiring complex toolchains, hardware-in-the-loop setups, and depending on non trivial inputs. This paper introduces a novel metamorphic testing technique tailored to transpilers. Instead of reasoning about the runtime behavior of compiled programs, our approach defines metamorphic relations directly over the source code produced by the transpiler. These relations capture a property that we call mutation consistency of the (transpiled) programs: mutation-style changes in the input DSL program must induce predictable and structurally consistent changes in the generated output. We implemented this idea in a tool, MCP-Tester, and evaluated it through a case study conducted in the context of a technology-transfer project. Our current empirical results indicate that the proposed approach can effectively reveal faults that would remain undetected with pure fuzzing.

</details>


### [143] [BRHC: Backend-driven Reactive Hypermedia Controls with a Statically Typed Kotlin DSL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29338)
*Fernando Miguel Carvalho,Paulo Carvalho,Leonel Correia,Ricardo Gomes,Juho Vepsäläinen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Kotlin HTML DSL的静态类型方法，用于构建后端驱动的响应式Web应用，通过扩展HtmlFlow DSL支持类型化的自定义HTML属性和信号绑定，几乎消除了对JavaScript的需求，同时提高了类型安全性并保持了前后端统一的编程模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编码工具使Web应用生成日益普遍，但带来了系统复杂性、长寿性和长期可维护性问题。前后端异构编程模型（多语言、多范式）导致逻辑重复和状态管理分散。现有的超媒体驱动应用（HDA）方法（如HTMX、Turbo Hotwire、Datastar）虽然将状态和交互逻辑移至服务器端，但仍通过自定义属性和计算表达式引入复杂性，且未能完全消除JavaScript。

Method: 提出一种基于Kotlin的HTML DSL（领域特定语言）的静态类型方法，用于后端驱动的响应式Web应用。具体包括：1) 扩展HtmlFlow Kotlin DSL，添加类型化的自定义HTML属性（即Datastar data-*属性）；2) 使用静态类型构建器实现基于信号的绑定；3) 通过响应式交互模式目录和Petclinic Spring MVC案例研究进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法几乎消除了对JavaScript的需求，同时提高了类型安全性，并保持了前后端统一的编程模型，通过后端驱动的响应式、信号中心架构桥接前后端。

Conclusion: 基于Kotlin HTML DSL的静态类型方法可以作为一种替代方案，在保持超媒体驱动应用优势的同时，更好地解决类型安全和编程模型同质化问题，为AI辅助生成Web应用的长期可维护性提供了新的思路。

Abstract: AI-assisted coding tools (e.g., Copilot, Cursor, Claude) are increasingly ubiquitous and enable rapid generation of web applications. However, this raises concerns regarding complexity, longevity and the long-term maintainability of generated systems. A key source of complexity is the heterogeneity between backend and frontend programming models, where multiple languages and paradigms are combined within a single application, often leading to duplicated logic and fragmented state management. To address this issue, recent approaches (e.g., HTMX, Turbo Hotwire, Datastar, etc.) follow the Hypermedia-Driven Application (HDA) model, positioning HTML as the primary communication medium between client and server. Unlike SPA-centric architectures, HDA systems shift the application state and interaction logic to the server, where backend-driven reactive signals synchronize with the client user interface. However, these approaches still introduce complexity through custom attributes and do not fully eliminate JavaScript, particularly in computed expressions. In this work, we propose a statically typed approach using a Kotlin-based HTML DSL (Domain-Specific Language) for backend-driven reactive web applications. We extend the HtmlFlow Kotlin DSL with typed custom HTML attributes (i.e., Datastar data-* attributes) and signal-based bindings using statically typed builders. We demonstrate the approach through a catalog of reactive interaction patterns and a Petclinic Spring MVC case study. The results indicate that the proposed approach can nearly eliminate the need for JavaScript while improving type safety and preserving a homogeneous programming model across frontend and backend, bridged through a backend-driven reactive, signal-centric architecture.

</details>


### [144] [AgenticRepair: Multi-Faceted Program Context Engineering for Agentic Vulnerability Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29422)
*Michael Fu,Qiyue Mei,Patanamon Thongtanunam,Kla Tantithamthavorn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgenticRepair是一个智能体驱动的漏洞修复框架，通过工程化三种关键程序上下文（代码结构、运行时执行、提交历史），协调多个LLM子智能体为修复智能体构建条件化记忆，在SEC-Bench基准上达到73%的成功率，远超最强基线29个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体AI方法在通用程序修复中表现良好，但漏洞修复需要比通用bug修复更丰富的程序上下文。安全工程师在实践中会常规性地组装这些上下文，但现有智能体方法并未进行此类工程化。论文识别出三个关键缺口：跨文件数据流和内存操作模式的代码结构上下文、揭示崩溃语义和内存来源的运行时执行上下文、恢复脆弱代码模式引入历史的提交历史上下文。

Method: AgenticRepair框架通过三个方面构建上下文：协调三个专门的LLM子智能体分别工程化代码结构上下文、运行时执行上下文和提交历史上下文；将这些上下文嵌入到专用修复子智能体的记忆中，用于上下文条件化的补丁合成；采用多智能体协作架构实现上下文工程与修复的分离。

Result: 在包含300个真实世界漏洞实例并使用sanitizer验证补丁的SEC-Bench基准上，AgenticRepair达到73%的修复成功率，比最强基线高出29%。消融研究表明三种上下文维度相互补充，且多智能体脚手架和基础模型容量各自发挥关键作用。

Conclusion: 论文确立多面程序上下文工程化作为智能体漏洞修复的有前景设计方向，证明代码结构、运行时执行和提交历史三种上下文维度的互补性，为未来智能体漏洞修复系统设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability repair aims to reduce the time and effort required to patch security flaws from a vulnerability triage report. Recent agentic AI approaches have shown promising results in automated program repair. However, vulnerability repair demands richer program context than general bug repair - context that security engineers routinely assemble in practice but that existing agentic approaches do not engineer. We identify three critical gaps: code-structure context capturing cross-file data flows and memory operation patterns, runtime-execution context revealing crash semantics and memory origins, and commit-history context recovering how fragile code patterns were introduced. We present AgenticRepair, an agentic vulnerability repair framework that addresses the gaps through multi-faceted program context engineering. AgenticRepair orchestrates three specialized LLM subagents to engineer the contexts, which are then embedded into the memory of a dedicated repair subagent for context-conditioned patch synthesis. Evaluated on SEC-Bench comprising 300 real-world instances with sanitizer-based patch verification, AgenticRepair achieves a 73% success rate, substantially outperforming the strongest baseline by 29%. Our ablation study confirms that the three context facets are mutually complementary, and that multi-agent scaffolding and base-model capacity each play an essential role. Collectively, these findings establish multi-faceted program context engineering as a promising design direction for agentic vulnerability repair.

</details>


### [145] [From Code Review to Code Critique: Intent, Drift, and Spotlight for AI-Generated Diffs at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29516)
*Chandra Maddila,Mashrur Rashik,Euna Mehnaz Khan,Smriti Jha,James Saindon,Nachi Nagappan,Peter C. Rigby*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ARCTIC，一个AI驱动的代码评审系统，通过意图预测、漂移检测和代码聚光灯三大核心能力重构代码评审流程，以应对AI编码代理产生的高容量代码与传统同行评审能力之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同行评审无法应对AI编码代理产生的大量代码；现有AI代码审查工具过度关注风格和最佳实践等低价值建议，而忽视了人工评审员最关心的正确性、安全性和性能问题。

Method: ARCTIC包含三个核心能力：（1）意图预测——从对话日志和元数据推断代码变更的原因；（2）漂移检测——通过反向翻译衡量开发者意图与代理输出之间的差异；（3）代码聚光灯——对diff中最值得人工审查的区域进行排序。这些能力基于从18,000条代码评审中提炼的六主题分类法。

Result: 离线评估显示：意图预测F1达0.86，漂移检测与人工标注者的序数一致性接近完美（QWK=0.907），聚光灯光照质量估计性能比基线AI评审者高2.4倍且token用量减少5倍。实验部署中，漂移评分使代码错位额外减少5.76分（p=0.026），意图预测获得90.2%批准率，自上线以来无缺陷归因于自我评审的diff。

Conclusion: ARCTIC通过聚焦人类评审最关心的高价值维度（正确性、安全性、性能），显著提升了AI辅助代码评审的质量和效率，为应对AI时代大规模代码审查提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AI coding agents are generating code at volumes that exceed the capacity of traditional peer review. At the same time, existing AI code review tools over-index on low-value suggestions such as style and best practices while under-indexing on the concerns human reviewers prioritize most: correctness, security, and performance. We present ARCTIC, an AI-powered Code Critique system that reframes code review around three capabilities: intent prediction, which infers why a change was made from conversation logs and metadata; drift detection, which measures divergence between the developer's intent and the agent's output via backtranslation; and code spotlight, which ranks the regions of a diff most warranting human scrutiny. We ground these capabilities in a six-theme taxonomy derived from 18,000 code reviews. Offline evaluation shows that intent prediction achieves 0.86 F1, drift detection reaches near-perfect ordinal agreement with human annotators (QWK = 0.907), and spotlight outperforms the baseline AI reviewer by 2.4x on quality estimation at 5x fewer tokens. In the experimental rollout, the drift scores reduces code misalignment by an additional 5.76 points (p = 0.026), intent prediction receives 90.2% approval, and zero defects have been attributed to self-reviewed diffs since launch.

</details>


### [146] [Students' Practices and Skills in the LLM-Era: "You Can't Outsource the Struggle and Still Get the Skill"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29519)
*Enne Rebeca Silva de Freitas,Gustavo Pinto,Danilo Monteiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析五个研究型subreddit的1,383条帖子，发现软件工程研究生在使用生成式AI工具时系统性外包了认知努力，导致既未获得预期研究结果，也未培养必要的研究能力。论文旨在识别这些缺失的AI相关技能，为研究生课程设计提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具已迅速融入软件工程研究生的日常工作中，但目前缺乏对研究生在实证研究中有效使用AI所需技能的系统认知。如果不了解这些技能需求，研究生项目无法培养学生适应LLM时代的研究能力，可能导致学生盲目的任务委托而缺乏必要的专业能力。

Method: 对来自五个研究型subreddit的1,383条帖子进行了质性分析，系统识别研究生在实证研究中与AI使用相关的技能缺口。

Result: 研究发现研究生系统性地将发展研究技能所需的认知努力外包给AI工具，最终既没有获得预期的结果，也没有培养出必要的研究能力。论文明确了这些缺失的具体技能。

Conclusion: 识别这些缺失的技能是迈向设计研究生课程的第一步，这些课程旨在教会学生如何与LLM协同工作而不被其取代。

Abstract: Generative AI tools have been rapidly learned in the daily workflow of graduate students in Software Engineering, but little is known about what AI-related skills they actually need for effective use in empirical research. Without this understanding, graduate programs cannot prepare students to conduct rig-orous research in the LLM era, risking creating a generation of researchers who delegate tasks without the necessary expertise. By analyzing 1,383 posts from five research-focused subreddits, we found that students systematically outsource the cognitive effort required to develop research skills and end up with neither the expected results nor the necessary competence. Naming these missing skills is the first step toward curricula that teach graduate students to work \emph{with} LLMs without being replaced by them.

</details>


### [147] [AuditCoder: Responsibility-Preserving Task Graphs for Auditable Code Generation and Bounded Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29529)
*Kangjie Huang,Chen Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AuditCoder提出了一种将程序与可审计的构造轨迹作为联合输出的代码生成框架。通过合同标注的任务图赋予每个承诺稳定的责任身份，在验证失败时用保守定位器映射证据到节点或依赖分支，并仅对局部区域进行有界修复。在APPS和ClassEval上取得了有竞争力的pass@1结果，同时在决策-代码追踪覆盖率和失败定位精度上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成器返回程序，但通常不保留构造记录，无法将失败与产生受影响代码的决策连接起来，也难以界定合理的修复范围。这种透明性和可审计性的缺失限制了失败诊断与精确修复的能力。

Method: 1) 在代码生成前，使用合同标注的任务图将稳定的责任身份分配给每个承诺、其拥有的实现、来源、验证证据及干预历史；2) 验证失败时，使用保守定位器将异构证据映射到具体节点或依赖分支（或放弃）；3) 执行有界修复，仅重新生成失败区域，同时重用冻结的其余部分。

Result: 在APPS上达到82.5%-83.0%的pass@1，相比未修复的图分解恢复了大部分损失，但落后AgentCoder 7.5-8.5个百分点。在ClassEval上达到75.0%-82.0%，优于CoT+重试但低于AgentCoder。对200条APPS记录的审计显示0.9725的任务-宏决策-代码追踪覆盖率；定位器为60个失败中的26个识别出有证据支持的节点或分支，其中17个局部修复通过。

Conclusion: 对于具有稳定、可局部测试边界任务，任务图不仅作为分解结构，还作为验证和修复的持久索引。这种可审计的构造追踪方法为代码生成提供了更可诊断、更可修复的路径，虽然整体性能仍略低于顶级基线，但在可追溯性和修复精度方面具有独特价值。

Abstract: Code generators return programs, but typically do not preserve the construction record needed to connect a failure to the decision that produced the affected code or to delimit a justified repair. We present AuditCoder, which treats the program and an auditable construction trace as joint outputs. Before code generation, a contract-annotated task graph assigns stable responsibility identities that remain attached to each commitment, its owned implementation, provenance, validation evidence, and intervention history. When validation fails, a conservative locator maps heterogeneous evidence to a node or dependency branch---or abstains---and bounded repair regenerates only that region while reusing the frozen complement. On APPS, \method{} reaches $82.5$--$83.0\%$ \texttt{pass@1}, recovering much of the loss caused by unrepaired graph decomposition but trailing AgentCoder by $7.5$--$8.5$ points. On ClassEval, it reaches $75.0$--$82.0\%$, outperforming CoT + retry while remaining below AgentCoder. A separate audit of 200 APPS records yields $0.9725$ task-macro decision--code trace coverage; the locator identifies an evidence-supported node or branch for 26 of 60 failures, and 17 of those localized repairs pass. For tasks with stable, locally testable boundaries, the graph functions not only as a decomposition structure but also as a persistent index for validation and repair.

</details>


### [148] [Improving the Understandability of Conceptual Models via Abstract Notation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29552)
*Amine Abbad-Andaloussi,Daniel Jeppe Schütt,Kasper Solhøj Jørgensen,Tobias van Deurs Lundsgaard,Hugo A. López*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语言无关的"抽象符号工程"方法，用于将概念建模中的低级构造配置替换为更高级、语义透明的构造。该方法包含模式识别、模式形式化、视觉符号设计和实证验证四个步骤，并在DCR图中实例化，生成了DeCleaR扩展，实证结果表明其优于标准DCR图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念建模中，领域级抽象往往需要通过低级构造来编码以满足语义一致性，导致模型难以理解。以往研究主要聚焦于改进单个构造的视觉表示，而忽略了重复出现的构造配置（模式）这一更高层次的问题。

Method: 提出抽象符号工程（abstract notation engineering）方法，包含四个步骤：模式识别、模式形式化、视觉符号设计、实证验证。将该方法应用于DCR图，针对常见工作流模式所需的复杂低级配置，设计了DeCleaR扩展，用紧凑的基于模式的抽象替换这些配置。

Result: 实证验证结果表明，DeCleaR相比标准DCR图在感知经验质量、实用质量和用户偏好方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 从单个构造转向重复出现的构造配置是提升概念模型可理解性的有效途径，抽象符号工程为这一方向提供了系统化的方法论。

Abstract: Conceptual modeling supports the design, analysis, and communication of the properties of complex systems, yet conceptual models can be difficult to understand when domain-level abstractions must be encoded through low-level constructs required mainly for semantic conformity. Prior work has mainly improved how existing individual constructs are visually represented. We shift the focus from individual constructs to recurring configurations of constructs, and propose abstract notation engineering as a language-agnostic method for replacing such configurations with higher-level, semantically transparent constructs. The method comprises pattern identification, pattern formalization, visual notation design, and empirical validation. We instantiate it for Dynamic Condition Response (DCR) graphs, where common workflow patterns require elaborate low-level configurations. The resulting extension, DeCleaR, replaces such configurations with compact pattern-based abstractions. The results of our empirical validation show that DeCleaR improves perceived empirical quality, pragmatic quality, and user preference over standard DCR graphs.

</details>


### [149] [Alteron: A Tool for Behavioral Regression Testing Across NLP Classifier Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29557)
*Shazzad Hossain,Proma Chowdhury,Mridha Md. Nafis Fuad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Alteron是一个用于检测NLP模型版本间行为回归的工具，采用蜕变测试方法，通过构造测试语料库并比较模型版本在蜕变转换输入上的表现来识别行为退化。在10个蜕变关系、4个模型版本和3次模型更新转换的评估中，识别出16个行为回归，其中11个为阻止发布级别的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基准指标无法全面捕捉NLP模型版本更新时的行为变化，现有研究主要关注单独测试模型而非在持续集成工作流中比较连续版本，需要一种能够检测跨版本行为回归的方法。

Method: 提出Alteron工具，基于蜕变测试框架：从标注的源样本构建测试语料库，对输入进行蜕变转换，然后比较不同模型版本在这类转换输入上的行为差异，从而识别行为回归。

Result: 在10个蜕变关系（MRs）、4个模型版本和3次模型更新场景的评估中，Alteron识别出16个行为回归，其中11个达到了阻止发布的严重程度。实验表明常见模型更新在保持整体任务性能的同时仍可能引入不良行为变化。

Conclusion: 跨模型版本的行为检查能够揭示聚合基准指标无法捕获的失败，Alteron作为开源工具为NLP模型的持续集成测试提供了有效的行为回归检测方案。

Abstract: Evaluating evolving Natural Language Processing (NLP) models is important for ensuring reliable behavior across updates, but standard benchmark metrics do not fully capture how model behavior changes across versions. Existing work has focused mainly on testing models in isolation rather than comparing successive versions in continuous integration workflows. We present Alteron, a tool for detecting behavioral regressions across NLP model versions with metamorphic testing. Alteron constructs a test corpus from labeled source examples and compares model versions on metamorphically transformed inputs. In an evaluation spanning 10 metamorphic relations (MRs), 4 model versions, and 3 model-update transitions, Alteron identified 16 behavioral regressions, 11 of which were release-blocking. The results show that common model updates can preserve overall task performance while still introducing undesirable behavior changes, and that behavioral checks across model versions can reveal failures that aggregate benchmark metrics alone do not capture. The tool is open-source and available at https://github.com/shazzad5709/alteron. A screencast demonstration is available at https://youtu.be/szwiWW5O4do.

</details>


### [150] [Educating the Agentic Engineer: Curricula, Collaboration, and Continuous Learning in the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29610)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体工程师"（agentic engineer）这一新兴职业原型，并系统性地构建了ACCEL教育框架（Agentic Competencies through Curricula, Collaboration, and Enduring Learning），包含五大能力支柱和三大交付向量（课程、协作、持续学习），旨在培养能够指导、验证和治理自主AI系统的工程师。文章强调这需要系统性的教育转型而非渐进式的课程修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式和智能体式AI正在从根本上改变软件和系统工程的性质——从以人类撰写工件为中心，转向以指导、验证和治理自主系统为中心。然而现有的工程教育体系尚未适应这一转变，且经验证据表明AI带来的收益分布不均且常被误判，因此亟需一个证据支撑的教育架构来培养能够胜任这一新角色的专业人才。

Method: 本文采用综合概念综合方法（integrative conceptual synthesis），跨越工程教育、计算教育、人机交互、人因学和学习科学等多个学科领域。基于代理理论（agency theory）、自动化信任研究以及AI辅助编程的实证研究，提出了ACCEL框架，涵盖五大能力支柱和三个交付向量，并设计了支架式课程、委托-验证教学循环、重新设计的评估体系、治理素养伦理整合等具体方案。

Result: 提出了完整的ACCEL教育框架，明确了智能体工程师的核心能力（意图规约、多智能体工作流编排、机器生成输出的批判性评估、伦理判断），并给出了从课程设计到评估改革的系统性教育转型路径。同时识别了关键风险：自动化偏差、技能退化、浅层参与和分散问责。

Conclusion: 培养智能体工程师需要系统性的教育转型，而非渐进式的课程调整。教学重心必须从"生产工件"转向"对日益自主的社会技术系统行使判断力"，这一转变涉及课程、协作和持续学习三个维度的全面重构。

Abstract: Generative and agentic artificial intelligence (AI) are reconfiguring software and systems engineering from a discipline centered on human authorship of artifacts to one focused on directing, verifying, and governing autonomous systems. This transition demands a new professional archetype, the \emph{agentic engineer}, whose enduring value lies in intent specification, orchestration of multi-agent workflows, critical evaluation of machine-generated outputs, and ethical judgment. This article presents an integrative conceptual synthesis across engineering education, computing education, human--AI interaction, human factors, and the learning sciences to derive an evidence-grounded educational architecture for this archetype. We introduce the ACCEL framework (Agentic Competencies through Curricula, Collaboration, and Enduring Learning), which organizes five competency pillars and maps them to three delivery vectors: curricula, collaboration, and continuous learning. Drawing on agency theory, trust-in-automation research, and empirical studies of AI-assisted programming, including evidence that AI benefits are unevenly realized and often misperceived, we propose a scaffolded curriculum, a delegation--verification pedagogical loop for human--AI teaming, redesigned assessment, governance-literate ethics integration, and alignment with current curricular guidelines and international AI competency frameworks. We identify key risks, including automation bias, deskilling, superficial engagement, and diffuse accountability, and conclude that educating the agentic engineer requires systemic transformation rather than incremental curricular change: instruction must shift from producing artifacts to exercising judgment over increasingly autonomous socio-technical systems.

</details>


### [151] [Reusing Past Repairs Through Hierarchical Trajectory Abstraction for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29658)
*Yisen Xu,Jiayuan Zhou,Ruiqi Pan,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: STAIR是一个将历史修复轨迹转化为层级化、可复用计划的框架，通过多粒度抽象树编码修复经验，并在新问题到来时选择相关计划节点定制后注入agent提示中。在SWE-bench Verified上，与Lingxi集成达到81.2% Pass@1（MiniMax M2.5）和79.2%（GPT-5），且计划可跨agent迁移，将mini-SWE-agent v2从75.8%提升至81.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM驱动的修复agent能够处理复杂的仓库级问题，但每个问题都被独立处理，此前修复过程中积累的程序性知识被丢弃，缺乏经验的积累与复用机制。

Method: STAIR将每个历史修复轨迹转换为多层级树结构，从细粒度的诊断动作到高层次的修复策略，在不同粒度上编码经验。当新问题到来时，从多个抽象层级选择相关计划节点，定制为可执行的针对性计划，并通过提示提供给agent。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，STAIR集成Lingxi达到81.2% Pass@1（使用MiniMax M2.5）和79.2%（使用GPT-5）。生成的计划可以跨agent泛化，无需修改代码就将结构不同的mini-SWE-agent v2从75.8%提升到81.0%。消融实验表明混合多个抽象层级优于任何单一层级，原始未抽象轨迹的迁移效果显著更差。

Conclusion: 多层级抽象的经验表示能够有效捕获和复用修复知识，跨agent迁移能力证明了计划本身的可泛化性，混合不同抽象层级是实现最佳效果的关键。

Abstract: Although LLM-driven repair agents can tackle complex, repository-level issues, they treat every issue independently and discard the procedural knowledge accumulated from previous repairs. We introduce STAIR, a framework that converts historical repair trajectories into hierarchical, reusable plans that can be adapted to steer future repairs. Each past trajectory is transformed into a multi-level tree that ranges from fine-grained diagnostic actions to high-level repair strategies, encoding experience at several granularities. When a new issue arrives, STAIR selects relevant plan nodes from multiple abstraction levels, tailors them into executable, issue-specific plans, and supplies them to the agent through its prompt. On SWE-bench Verified, STAIR integrated with Lingxi reaches 81.2% Pass@1 using MiniMax M2.5 and 79.2% using GPT-5. The generated plans also generalize across agents: without any code change, they lift the Pass@1 of a structurally different agent, mini-SWE-agent v2, from 75.8% to 81.0%. Ablation experiments further show that mixing multiple abstraction levels surpasses any single level and that raw, unabstracted trajectories transfer substantially worse.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Beyond Byzantine: An Organizational Consensus Algorithm for Self-Interested Agents Under Information Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28957)
*Jiawei Zhang,Jianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出组织共识算法（OCA），一种面向组织内部协商场景的机制设计框架，将部门间冲突建模为不完全信息动态博弈，采用代币质押、异常触发挑战机制和置信加权共识规则，并通过延迟可验证结果的追溯惩罚机制来替代即时全序共识，以降低协调开销并抑制结构偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布式共识协议将节点简单分为诚实或拜占庭两类，但组织结构中的部门代理通常不适用这种二元分类——他们具有有限理性、自利偏好并在信息不对称下运作。现有共识框架无法有效建模和应对此类复杂的组织协商场景。

Method: OCA将部门间冲突建模为不完全信息动态博弈，整合三个核心机制：(1) 内部代币质押（token staking）、(2) 异常触发挑战机制（exception-triggered challenge）、(3) 置信加权共识规则（confidence-weighted consensus）。同时采用基于延迟可验证结果的追溯惩罚系统，而非强制即时全序共识。

Result: 基于Python仿真原型，在多种组织规模和噪声环境下，OCA报告较低的协调开销、更高的信息上报率，并在噪声环境中保持有界福利损失。

Conclusion: OCA在仿真中表现良好，但结果严格依赖于仿真模型设定，不能自行证明一般性的诚实均衡（truthful equilibrium）的存在。

Abstract: Traditional distributed consensus protocols classify nodes as either honest-but-faulty or actively malicious (Byzantine). However, in organizational structures, departmental agents rarely fit this binary. Instead, they exhibit bounded rationality and self-interested preferences while operating under asymmetric information. This paper presents the Organizational Consensus Algorithm (OCA), a mechanism design framework tailored for internal negotiation and decision coordination. OCA models inter-departmental conflict as an incomplete information dynamic game, integrating internal token staking, an exception-triggered challenge mechanism, and confidence-weighted consensus rules. Rather than enforcing instantaneous total ordering, OCA leverages a retrospective penalty system driven by delayed verifiable outcomes to deter structural bias and reduce exhaustive coordination overhead. A Python simulation prototype was developed to evaluate OCA. Across independent trials with varying organizational scales, OCA reports lower coordination overhead, higher informative reporting rates, and bounded welfare loss in noisy environments. Crucially, these results remain conditional on the stated simulation model and do not by themselves establish a general truthful equilibrium.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [RareSense: Rarity-Aware Similarity Search for Anomaly Retrieval in Transactional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28879)
*Sidahmed Benabderrahmane,Talal Rahwan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RareSense是一个面向稀疏集合型异常数据的稀有性感知相似度框架。它通过挖掘最小稀有项集并推导稀有关联规则，将对象映射为稀疏规则配置文件，并使用加权Jaccard相似度进行比较，从而在相似性搜索中显式捕获有意义的高阶共现模式，优于传统的原子级度量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相似度度量（如Jaccard、余弦、Hamming）在稀疏集合型数据上搜索时，往往被频繁的背景属性主导，因为这些度量基于原子级重叠比较对象。IDF加权虽能部分缓解，但仍是逐项的，无法显式表示更有信息量的高阶共现模式。

Method: RareSense框架包含四个步骤：(1)挖掘最小稀有项集作为中间结构；(2)推导可靠的稀有关联规则；(3)将对象映射为稀疏的稀有规则配置文件；(4)使用加权Jaccard相似度进行比较。规则权重综合了逆支持度、置信度、提升度、结构复杂度和稳定性。理论证明IDF加权Jaccard是RareSense的受限单例特例，且诱导的距离为原始对象上的伪度量及等价类上的度量。

Result: 在四个基准家族（覆盖网络安全和通用类别型数据）的实验表明，RareSense在查询条件化检索的宏平均性能上达到所有相似度度量中的最高值。统计分析显示总体存在显著差异，校正配对比较中RareSense优于原子基线。在全局异常排序任务中，RareSense达到最高观测宏平均性能，并与多个强专用检测器在统计上相当。

Conclusion: RareSense通过稀有高阶共现模式驱动相似性计算，在共享可重复稀有高阶结构的异常场景下表现出最强的增益，但性能提升是工作负载依赖的。该框架为稀疏异常数据提供了比传统原子级度量更优的相似性搜索方案。

Abstract: Similarity search over sparse set-valued data is often dominated by frequent background attributes because classical measures such as Jaccard, cosine, and Hamming compare objects through atomic overlap. IDF (Inverse document frequency) weighting partially reduces this effect but remains atom-wise and cannot explicitly represent informative higher-order co-occurrences. We introduce RareSense, a rarity-aware similarity framework for sparse transactional anomaly data. RareSense mines minimal rare itemsets as intermediate structures, derives reliable rare association rules, maps objects into sparse rare-rule profiles, and compares them using weighted Jaccard similarity. Rule weights combine inverse support, confidence, lift, structural complexity, and stability, so that neighborhoods are determined by shared rare evidence rather than uniform feature overlap. We show that IDF-weighted Jaccard is a restricted singleton case of RareSense, and that the induced distance is a pseudometric on the original objects and a metric over equivalence classes defined by identical rule profiles. Experiments across four benchmark families spanning cybersecurity and general categorical domains show that RareSense attains the highest observed macro-average query-conditioned retrieval performance among the evaluated similarity measures. The statistical analysis indicates significant overall differences, with corrected paired comparisons favoring RareSense over the atomic baselines. The gains remain workload-dependent and are strongest when anomalies share repeatable rare higher-order structure. For global anomaly ranking, RareSense achieves the highest observed macro-average performance while remaining statistically comparable to several strong dedicated detectors.

</details>


### [154] [LLM-Based Generative Retrieval for Snapchat Content Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28895)
*Liam Collins,Jiwen Ren,Donald Loveland,Bhuvesh Kumar,Clark Mingxuan Ju,Xuan Guo,Mo Li,Alvin Hou,Yi Cui,Peng Yang,Jian Wang,Saud Afzal Shafi,Nga Than,Ruiming Lu,Wenfeng Zhuo,Dongheng Li,Lili Zhang,Mingtao Zhang,Jinchao Ye,Vincent Xue,Chunhui Zhu,Neil Shah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SnapLGR，一个部署在Snapchat短视频推荐系统中的基于大语言模型的生成式检索系统。该系统通过语义标识符（SIDs）、PPR协同对比学习、继续预训练（CPT）以及TensorRT-LLM加速的波束搜索和去中心化worker-loop架构，解决了将预训练LLM转化为生产级生成式检索器面临的项目词汇表和延迟成本挑战。在线A/B测试显示，相比TIGER基线，View Time提升0.37%，Time Spent提升0.09%，Deep Sessions提升0.18%，Deep Sessions Unique User提升0.11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练大语言模型作为检索引擎具有丰富的语义先验、强大的序列建模能力和良好的扩展性，但要将其部署为生产环境的生成式检索器面临两大挑战：一是模型需要学习预训练阶段不存在的内部项目词汇表；二是需要在严格的延迟和成本约束下生成有效的项目标识符。

Method: 系统围绕三个核心设计：1）从多模态项目嵌入构建语义标识符（SIDs），并通过PPR（个性化PageRank）协同参与对比学习增强，改善码本利用率和减少冲突，注入协同信号；2）在监督微调（SFT）之前使用继续预训练（CPT）为引入的SID token打底；3）通过TensorRT-LLM CUDA支持的波束搜索和去中心化worker-loop架构实现高效推理服务。

Result: 在线A/B测试中，SnapLGR相比现有TIGER风格的生成式检索基线，View Time提升0.37%，Time Spent提升0.09%，Deep Sessions提升0.18%，Deep Sessions Unique User提升0.11%。此外，研究者在固定tokenizer下分解离线差距，量化了模型架构、扩展和预训练各自的贡献。

Conclusion: 成功部署生产级SnapLGR需要在表示学习、词汇表打底和高效训练/服务三方面进行联合设计，三者缺一不可。该系统证明了LLM生成式检索在生产环境中的可行性和实际收益。

Abstract: Pretrained large language models (LLMs) are promising retrieval engines because they combine rich semantic priors, strong sequence modeling capabilities, and favorable scaling behavior. However, turning a pretrained LLM into a generative retriever in production deployment raises several challenges: the model must learn an internal item vocabulary that was absent from pretraining, and generate valid item identifiers under strict latency and cost constraints. We address these challenges through the design and launch of SnapLGR, an LLM-based generative retrieval system for short-video recommendation at Snapchat. The system is built around three main designs. First, we construct semantic identifiers (SIDs) from multimodal item embeddings and enhance them with Personalized PageRank (PPR)-based co-engagement contrastive learning, resulting in improved codebook utilization, reduced collisions, and infused collaborative signal. Second, we use continued pretraining (CPT) to ground the introduced SID tokens before supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on user interaction sequences. Third, we make SnapLGR serving practical through TensorRT-LLM CUDA-backed beam search and a decentralized worker-loop architecture. In a live A/B test, the launched system increased View Time by 0.37%, Time Spent by 0.09%, Deep Sessions by 0.18%, and Deep Sessions Unique User by 0.11% relative to the existing TIGER-style generative retrieval baseline. We then decompose this offline gap under a fixed tokenizer and quantify the gains due to model architecture, scaling, and pretraining. Overall, our deployment shows that successful production SnapLGR requires joint design across representation learning, vocabulary grounding, and efficient training and serving.

</details>


### [155] [TransX: Scaling Transformer-based Recommendation via Behavioral and Serving Stream Crossings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28940)
*Da Xu,Liyan Fang,Divya Venugopalan,Sunny Hsu,Xukai Wang,Rishav Roy Chowdhury,Cindy Liang,Nishant Satya Lakshmikanth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TransX 是一个面向生产的编码器-解码器推荐架构，将推荐重新定义为序列到序列的动作转导问题，显式解耦行为流建模与服务事件建模，并通过跨注意力机制和摊销服务策略实现低延迟、高吞吐的部署，在 LinkedIn 上取得 CTR +6.0%、转化 +4.4% 的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业推荐系统越来越多地采用基于 Transformer 的序列模型，并将推荐视为在统一单体用户序列上的下一个 token 预测。然而，将长期用户行为和实时服务事件等异构数据源压缩成单一 token 流，会模糊它们不同的因果角色和时间特征，导致建模效率低下以及训练和服务成本升高。

Method: TransX 采用生产导向的编码器-解码器架构，将推荐重构为序列到序列的动作转导问题。它显式解耦行为流建模与实时服务事件建模，通过近线行为编码与实时服务表示之间的可扩展跨注意力来调节下一动作解码。同时还协同设计了摊销服务策略，结合增量行为编码和逐请求键值缓存，使服务延迟对行为序列长度不敏感。

Result: 在 LinkedIn 推荐系统上的大量离线实验和大型在线 A/B 测试表明，TransX 持续优于最先进的 DLRM 和序列基线模型，CTR 提升 +6.0%，转化增益 +4.4%，同时维持与现有生产模型相当的服务成本，协同设计的服务策略将在线计算量减少了约 80%。

Conclusion: TransX 通过显式解耦异构数据源并采用协同设计的摊销服务策略，在保持生产级服务成本的同时显著提升了推荐性能，验证了序列到序列动作转导范式在工业推荐系统中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems (RecSys) increasingly adopt Transformer-based sequence models, with an emerging paradigm that frames recommendation as next-token prediction over a unified monolithic user sequence. However, collapsing heterogeneous data sources -- such as long-term user behaviors and real-time serving events -- into a single monolithic token stream that obscures their distinct causal roles and temporal characteristics, leading to inefficient modeling and elevated training and serving costs. We propose TransX, a production-oriented encoder-decoder architecture that reformulates recommendation as a sequence-to-sequence action transduction problem. TransX explicitly decouples behavior-stream modeling from serving-event modeling and conditions next-action decoding on scalable cross-attention between nearline behavior encodings and real-time serving representations. To enable low-latency, high-QPS deployment, TransX is co-designed with an amortized serving strategy that combines incremental behavior encoding with per-request key-value caching, rendering serving latency insensitive to behavior sequence length. Extensive offline experiments and large-scale online A/B tests on LinkedIn's recommender systems show that TransX consistently outperforms state-of-the-art DLRMs and sequential baselines, and delivers substantial CTR lift (+6.0%) and conversion gain (+4.4%) while maintaining serving costs comparable to existing production models where our co-designed serving strategy reduces online computation by approximately 80%.

</details>


### [156] [Don't Contrast the Impossible: Region-Constrained Batching for Contrastive User Modeling on a Local Community Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28971)
*Seungho Han,Byeongchang Kim,Jin Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出区域约束批量采样（RCBS）方法，解决本地社区平台（如Karrot）中对比学习因地理限制产生的"不可能负样本"问题。RCBS构建区域同质的mini-batch，让用户只与可行区域内的物品进行对比，从而引入更难、更有信息量的负样本，在离线评估和在线A/B测试中均一致提升了用户表示质量及多个业务场景（主页排序、检索、展示广告排序）的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推荐系统中广泛使用对比学习进行用户建模，标准in-batch负采样隐式假设"任何用户可以看到任何物品"，但在本地社区平台（如Karrot）上，曝光受地理约束，许多用户-物品对在设计上就不可能发生，却仍被当作负样本参与训练，稀释了对比学习信号。

Method: 提出Region-Constrained Batch Sampling (RCBS)，一种简单有效的批采样方法，通过构建区域同质的mini-batch，使用户主要与可能看到的物品进行对比，用可行负样本替代不可能负样本，从而在现实曝光约束下自然地引入更难、更有信息量的负样本。

Result: 在离线评估和在线A/B测试中，RCBS一致性地提升了用户表示质量，并增强了主页信息流排序、检索和展示广告排序效果。学到的用户嵌入已在生产环境中跨多个应用部署。

Conclusion: RCBS有效解决了本地社区平台中对比学习的地理约束负样本问题，通过区域约束的采样机制显著提升了用户表示质量，并已成功在生产系统中部署验证。

Abstract: Contrastive learning is widely used for user modeling in large-scale recommender systems, where standard in-batch negatives implicitly assume universal exposure that any user can be shown any item. On local community platforms such as Karrot, however, exposure is geographically constrained; many user-item pairs are impossible by design yet still treated as negatives during training, diluting the contrastive learning signal. We address this impossible negatives problem and propose Region-Constrained Batch Sampling (RCBS), a simple yet effective batching method that constructs region-homogeneous mini-batches so that users are contrasted primarily against items they could feasibly see. By replacing impossible negatives with feasible ones, RCBS naturally introduces harder and more informative negatives under realistic exposure constraints. With offline evaluations and online A/B tests, we show that RCBS consistently improves user representation quality and consequently enhances home feed ranking, retrieval, and display ads ranking. The resulting user embeddings have been deployed in production across various applications.

</details>


### [157] [Think2Go: Generative Next POI Recommendation with LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28997)
*Zhuang Zhuang,Shanshan Feng,Hangwei Qian,Mingqi Yang,Heng Qi,Yanming Shen,Baocai Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Think2Go，一个生成式下一POI推荐框架，通过将监督微调（SFT）与基于强化学习（RL）的推理统一在同一架构中，联合优化记忆与自适应推理，并引入两种优势加权机制（基于核密度估计的提示词认知不确定性加权和基于奖励最大化的优势缩放）来校准策略优化，从而在测试时通过计算扩展探索多样化的时空模式，提升对语义ID的理解和推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下一POI推荐方法主要依赖浅层上下文信息和手工特征交互，但用户移动模式固有的稀疏性和复杂性限制了非推理模型捕捉深层意图的能力；而LLMs因缺乏对语义ID（SIDs）的深度理解且SIDs单独训练时表现不佳。本研究旨在解决这两类方法的局限性，通过测试时计算扩展和联合优化记忆与推理来提升推荐性能。

Method: Think2Go框架的核心方法包括：(1) 将SFT和RL-based推理统一在单一架构中，实现记忆与自适应推理的联合优化；(2) 提出两种优势加权机制——基于核密度估计的提示词认知不确定性加权（评估查询与用户历史的时空周期模式对齐度，在高不确定性下促进探索）和基于最大奖励归一化的奖励感知优势缩放（调整更新幅度，提升训练稳定性）；(3) 通过联合校准形成隐式课程学习策略，实现细粒度、实例感知的策略更新。

Result: 论文通过将SFT和RL推理统一，结合双重优势加权机制实现了对SID表示理解的增强和多样化时空模式的探索。该框架通过隐式课程学习策略防止熵塌缩，支持稳健探索，在训练稳定性和对噪声信号的鲁棒性方面均有改进。

Conclusion: Think2Go通过统一记忆与推理的生成式框架及精细化的策略校准机制，有效解决了非推理模型和LLM在下一POI推荐中的各自局限性，为将测试时计算扩展应用于序列推荐任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation task focuses on mining user behavioral preference patterns from historical check-ins to provide personalized suggestions for the next destination. Existing methods primarily rely on shallow contextual information and handcrafted feature interactions to predict the next POI. However, the inherent sparsity and complexity of user mobility patterns limit the computational capacity of non-reasoning models to capture deep intent, while large language models (LLMs) perform suboptimally because they lack a deep understanding of semantic IDs (SIDs) when SIDs are trained separately. To address these limitations, we propose Think2Go, a novel generative next POI recommendation framework, which enhances the model's comprehension of SID representations and explores diverse spatial-temporal patterns via test-time computational scaling. We unify supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL)-based reasoning within a single architecture, enabling joint optimization of memorization and adaptive reasoning to better retain user behavior patterns while exploring diverse user preferences. To further calibrate policy optimization in adaptive reasoning, we propose two advantage weighting mechanisms that integrate (1) prompt epistemic uncertainty, estimated via kernel density methods to assess the spatial-temporal periodic pattern alignment between queries and user history, promoting increased exploration under high epistemic uncertainty; and (2) reward-informed advantage scaling, captured by normalizing rewards against their maxima to adapt update magnitudes, thereby improving training stability and mitigating overfitting to noisy signals. This joint calibration forms an implicit curriculum learning strategy, delivering fine-grained, instance-aware policy updates that prevent entropy collapse and support robust exploration.

</details>


### [158] [PaletteID: Prototype-Composed Semantic Identifiers for Multimodal CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29000)
*Huanyu Liu,Baining Chen,Hui Liu,Zengyang Li,Ziyi Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PaletteID（PID），一种基于原型的语义标识符方法，用于多模态点击率（CTR）预测。PID受调色板色彩组合启发，使用紧凑的代表性原型项目作为语义锚点，通过Semantic Quality-Aware Determinantal Point Process（SQ-DPP）构建原型调色板，并为每个目标项目检索和聚合语义相关的原型，形成丰富的语义表示。实验表明PID在CTR预测上持续改进，对长尾项目收益更大，且比现有残差SID方法更稳健和可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义标识符（SID）的多模态推荐方法存在两大局限：1）码本分配无法保留语义相关性，且丢失了原始嵌入空间的细粒度连续信号；2）残差码路径高度依赖前缀码，限制了层级标识符的表示可扩展性，导致性能提升有限。

Method: 1）提出PaletteID（PID）原型语义标识符，受调色板颜色组合启发，用紧凑的代表性原型项目作为语义锚点桥接预训练多模态内容空间和推荐模型；2）设计Semantic Quality-Aware Determinantal Point Process（SQ-DPP）构建原型调色板，同时考虑局部内容密度和全局语义多样性；3）对每个目标项目检索语义相关的原型序列并聚合为PID表示，实现丰富且互补的语义建模。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的大量实验表明：PID在CTR预测上持续优于现有方法，对长尾项目提升更大；PID产生更稳健的标识符分配，且比现有残差SID方法具有更可解释的令牌语义。

Conclusion: PaletteID通过原型化语义标识符有效解决了现有SID方法语义相关性丢失和表示可扩展性受限的问题，在多模态CTR预测中取得了更好的性能、稳健性和可解释性。

Abstract: Multimodal information can improve the accuracy of click-through rate (CTR) prediction and effectively alleviate item cold-start and long-tail problems. Recent studies commonly discretize pretrained multimodal embeddings into semantic identifiers (SIDs), allowing the model to learn task-specific semantic representations for recommendation. However, existing methods still provide limited gains due to two major limitations. First, codebook assignment fails to preserve semantic relevance and discards fine-grained continuous signals in the original embedding space. Second, the residual code paths are highly dependent on prefix codes, which limits the effective representational scalability of hierarchical identifiers. To address these issues, we propose PaletteID (PID), a prototype-based semantic identifier. Inspired by palette-based color composition, PID uses a compact set of representative prototype items as semantic anchors to bridge pretrained multimodal content space and recommendation models. Specifically, we first construct a prototype palette with Semantic Quality-Aware Determinantal Point Process (SQ-DPP), which jointly considers local content density and global semantic diversity. Then, for each target item, PID retrieves a sequence of semantically related prototypes and aggregates them into an informative PID representation, enabling rich and complementary semantic modeling. Extensive experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that PID consistently improves CTR prediction and yields larger gains for long-tail items. PID also produces more robust identifier assignments and provides more interpretable token semantics than existing residual SID methods.

</details>


### [159] [EvoReason: Self-Evolving Reasoning Primitive-Guided On-Policy Distillation for Latent Reasoning in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29010)
*Zhuang Zhuang,Zhipeng Wei,Rongfeng Guo,Shijie Li,Peng Zhao,Jie Chen,Fei Pan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoReason，一个自演进的潜在推理框架，通过原语引导的on-policy蒸馏，将显式推理监督与学生的潜在推理空间自适应对齐，从而提升生成式推荐系统的推理迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在推理方法直接蒸馏原始CoT轨迹到潜在表示中，但推荐场景中的推理轨迹包含冗余表达和不稳定的推理路径，导致原始CoT监督不足以学习可迁移的潜在推理表示。

Method: EvoReason包含三个核心组件：(1) 从高质量智能体推荐轨迹中提取可复用的推理原语作为伪工具；(2) 赋予教师原语感知推理能力，生成结构化、低冗余、高一致性的CoT监督；(3) 引入自演进的on-policy蒸馏机制，使原语引导的推理过程根据学生的潜在推理结果持续演化，形成闭环协同进化。

Result: 通过闭环协同进化机制，策略更新持续根据潜在推理结果改进潜在推理行为，实现逐渐对齐的CoT监督和更有效的推理迁移。

Conclusion: EvoReason通过自适应对齐显式推理监督与潜在推理空间，解决了原始CoT监督在推荐推理场景中的次优问题，为延迟敏感部署下的潜在推理推荐提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative recommendation benefits from reasoning-enhanced inference, and latent reasoning offers an efficient paradigm by encoding intermediate reasoning processes into compact continuous representations for latency-sensitive deployment. Despite its efficiency, existing latent reasoning approaches typically rely on directly distilling raw chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories into latent representations, assuming that textual reasoning traces provide sufficient supervision. However, recommendation reasoning trajectories contain diverse reasoning processes with redundant expressions and unstable reasoning paths, making raw CoT supervision suboptimal for learning transferable latent reasoning representations. To address this challenge, we propose EvoReason, a self-evolving latent reasoning framework that adaptively aligns explicit reasoning supervision with the student's latent reasoning space through primitive-guided on-policy distillation. First, EvoReason extracts reusable reasoning primitives from high-quality agentic recommendation trajectories, where each primitive captures an essential reasoning behavior and serves as a pseudo-tool for structured teacher reasoning. Then, based on these primitives, we equip the teacher with primitive-aware reasoning capabilities, enabling it to generate structured CoT supervision with reduced redundancy and improved consistency. Finally, during latent reasoning optimization, EvoReason introduces a self-evolving on-policy distillation mechanism, where the primitive-guided reasoning process evolves according to the student's latent reasoning outcomes. Through this closed-loop co-evolution, policy updates continuously improve latent reasoning behaviors is refined according to the resulting latent reasoning outcomes, enabling progressively better-aligned CoT supervision and more effective reasoning transfer.

</details>


### [160] [Reproducing LightMem: Naive RAG Is Just as Good for Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29104)
*Yongjie Zhou,Shuai Wang,Bevan Koopman,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文复现了LightMem（一种轻量级对话记忆管理方法），并将其与直接从原始用户轮次检索的Naive RAG进行对比。研究发现：检索器选择是性能变化的主要来源（仅更换检索器可使准确率从58.1%提升至75.5%），构造的记忆并不总是优于原始轮次检索，且记忆构造会丢失部分答案相关信息。总体而言，LightMem提供的是上下文效率的权衡而非普遍优势，其价值取决于检索器和token预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话智能体需要从早期交互中获取信息，但完整对话历史成本高。LightMem声称轻量且高效，但先前研究仅使用单一检索器评估，未检验其对检索器选择的敏感度，也未检查记忆构造是否丢失答案相关信息。本研究旨在通过复现和系统对比回答这些问题。

Method: 作者复现LightMem，并与Naive RAG（直接从原始用户轮次检索）进行对比。主要实验包括：1）在不同检索器条件下测试LightMem的性能变化；2）在匹配检索深度下比较LightMem与Naive RAG；3）在不同答案token预算下进行对比；4）通过Oracle评估检查记忆构造是否丢失答案相关信息。

Result: 复现了LightMem的主要配置趋势，但发现：1）检索器选择导致巨大性能差异（58.1%至75.5%）；2）构造记忆不始终优于原始轮次检索；3）Naive RAG在匹配检索深度下通常表现更好，LightMem仅在严格token预算下占优；4）Oracle评估证实记忆构造移除了部分答案相关信息。

Conclusion: LightMem的优势并非普遍的，而是体现为上下文效率与检索效果的权衡。其价值依赖检索器和可用token预算。未来工作应关注检索、重排序、查询公式化及其与原始和构造记忆表示的交互。

Abstract: Long-term conversational agents require access to information from earlier interactions, such as a user's preferences, past requests, or previously mentioned facts. Repeatedly providing the full dialogue history can be expensive as conversations grow, so many memory approaches instead transform past interactions into compact entries that can be retrieved when needed. LightMem is a recent lightweight memory-management approach that reports strong effectiveness while maintaining relatively low construction cost. However, it still relies on a separate constructed memory representation and is evaluated with only one retriever, leaving unclear how sensitive its results are to retriever choice and whether memory construction discards answer-relevant information.
  In this study, we reproduce LightMem and compare it with Naive RAG, which retrieves directly from raw user turns. We recover LightMem's main configuration trend, but find that retriever choice is a major source of performance variation: changing only the retriever over a fixed LightMem store shifts answer accuracy from 58.1% to 75.5%. Constructed memories also do not consistently outperform raw-turn retrieval. Naive RAG generally performs better at matched retrieval depths, whereas LightMem performs better mainly under tight answering-token budgets. Oracle evaluation further shows that memory construction removes some answer-relevant information.
  Overall, LightMem offers a context-efficiency trade-off rather than a general advantage over Naive RAG. Its value depends on the retriever and available token budget, motivating future work on retrieval, reranking, query formulation, and their interaction with raw and constructed memory representations.

</details>


### [161] [GALA: Generative Aligned Learning for Adaptive Multimodal Representation in the Taobao Shangou Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29213)
*Jiping Liu,Zhongmin Zhang,Zisen Sang,Zhijia Fang,Tao Ouyang,Ma Jiang,Shaopeng Liang,Zeyang Hou,Guodong Cao,Jia Jia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GALA是一个针对外卖推荐系统的三阶段多模态对齐管线，通过引入中间"生成式RL对齐"阶段，用行为数据构建多模态预训练数据并基于转化奖励进行优化，弥合预训练与微调之间的差距，已在淘宝闪购部署，服务超2亿日活用户，离线AUC提升+0.12/+0.20，在线订单量提升0.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外卖推荐系统虽整合了图像、文本和用户交互等多模态信号，但主流两阶段方法中，图像-文本编码器的内容语义预训练与行为驱动的排序模型相互分离，导致语义理解与用户行为模式之间缺乏对齐，无法有效融合异构模态并适应不断演化的用户意图。

Method: GALA采用三阶段流程：第一阶段基于搜索日志的查询-图像-文本三元组进行行为感知的三元组预训练，捕获用户意图和内容偏好；第二阶段引入新颖的中间"生成式RL对齐"阶段，通过GRPO（基于转化的奖励驱动优化）动态细化多模态嵌入，弥合预训练与微调之间的差距；第三阶段通过自适应门控和混合损失整合多模态与ID嵌入，在长期ID主导训练下保留多模态贡献。

Result: 与SOTA方法相比，离线AUC提升+0.12/+0.20，PCOC指标更优；大规模在线A/B测试显示订单量提升0.55%，验证了在工业规模下的有效性和对不同需求模式的鲁棒性。系统已在淘宝闪购生产环境部署，服务超2亿日活用户。

Conclusion: GALA通过引入生成式RL对齐作为中间阶段，有效弥合了多模态预训练与行为驱动微调之间的差距，实现了多模态信号与用户行为模式的动态对齐，并在大规模工业场景中验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems in food delivery increasingly leverage multimodal signals, including images, text, and user interaction histories, to enhance user experience, yet effective fusion of these heterogeneous modalities remains challenging, hindering both the joint modeling of multimodal signals and adaptation to evolving user intent. In mainstream two-stage approaches, the separation between content-semantic pretraining of image-text encoders and behavior-driven ranking models limits alignment between semantic understanding and user behavior patterns. To address these issues, we present GALA, a three-stage pipeline whose core innovation lies in an intermediate "generative RL alignment" stage that constructs multimodal pretraining data from user behavior and refines it via conversion-based rewards, effectively bridging the pretraining-fine-tuning gap to align with downstream objectives. GALA comprises three stages: first, behavior-aware triplet pretraining on query-image-text pairs from search logs to early capture user intent and content preferences; second, a novel intermediate stage that refines multimodal embeddings through reward-driven optimization (GRPO) to dynamically align them with user behavior and bridge the pretraining-fine-tuning gap; and finally, integration of multimodal and ID embeddings via adaptive gating with a hybrid loss, preserving multimodal contributions under long-term ID-dominant training. GALA has been deployed in the production environment at Taobao Shangou, serving over 200 million daily active users. Compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, it delivers consistent offline gains of +0.12/+0.20 AUC along with better PCOC metrics. Large-scale online A/B tests further report a 0.55 percent increase in order volume, confirming GALA's effectiveness at industrial scale and its robustness across diverse demand patterns.

</details>


### [162] [RecHarness: A Bandit-Routed Agentic Harness for Self-Evolving Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29241)
*Haoran Ling,Yuecheng Li,Zeyu Song,Jing Yao,Shuwen Kang,Chi Lu,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RecHarness，一种基于Bandit路由的智能体框架，用于自动化推荐模型优化。该框架将优化过程分为两步：Bandit路由根据历史验证反馈选择下一修改方向，LLM在该方向内生成具体优化假设和可执行代码修改。通过跳跃盆地机制应对局部停滞，实现在有限实验预算下的稳定性能提升，并在大规模短视频广告平台的线上A/B测试中取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐模型的优化仍高度依赖工程师人工迭代尝试架构、目标和训练策略的修改。虽然LLM智能体可以自动化这一试错过程，但让LLM同时负责选择修改方向和生成具体假设，在有限的实验预算下往往导致搜索不稳定。因此需要一种更稳定的自动化优化框架。

Method: RecHarness将优化过程解耦为两个步骤：1) Bandit路由器根据历史验证反馈选择下一个修改方向（作为一个多臂老虎机问题）；2) LLM在选定方向内生成具体的优化假设和可执行的代码修改。同时引入跳跃盆地（jump-basin）机制，当局部编辑停滞时激活结构性跳转臂，以支持长期探索并避免陷入局部最优。

Result: 在多个推荐任务、数据集和模型骨干上，RecHarness相比LLM推理搜索实现了更稳定的性能提升，且更有效地利用了有限的试验预算。在大型短视频广告平台上进行为期7天的在线A/B测试中，选出的候选方案使ADVV提升2.084%、Revenue提升0.534%、Exposure提升0.559%。

Conclusion: 通过将方向选择（由Bandit完成）与假设生成（由LLM完成）分离，RecHarness在自动化推荐模型优化中实现了比纯LLM推理搜索更稳定、更高效的表现，证明了分解优化决策过程对提升搜索稳定性与预算利用率的有效性。

Abstract: Optimizing modern recommender models still depends heavily on engineers manually iterating over architectural, objective, and training-strategy changes. While LLM-based agents can automate this trial-and-error process, allowing the LLM to both select modification directions and generate concrete hypotheses often leads to unstable search under limited experiment budgets. Inspired by the above challenge, we propose RecHarness, a Bandit-Routed Agentic Harness for automated recommender model optimization. RecHarness separates the optimization process into two steps: a bandit router selects the next modification direction according to historical validation feedback, while the LLM generates a concrete optimization hypothesis and executable code edit within the selected direction. To sustain long-horizon exploration, RecHarness uses a jump-basin mechanism to activate a structural-jump arm when local edits stagnate. Across multiple recommendation tasks, datasets, and model backbones, RecHarness achieves more stable performance improvements and uses limited trial budgets more effectively than LLM-reasoning search. During a 7-day online A/B test on a large-scale short-video advertising platform, the selected candidate improves ADVV by 2.084%, Revenue by 0.534%, and Exposure by 0.559%. Code is available at https://github.com/6lyc/RecHarness.

</details>


### [163] [Language Models Agree With Each Other, Not With Readers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29274)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究通过一个非人为设计的参考基准——120个网络文档上2523名读者自主标注的标记集——来测量语言模型的同质化（homogenisation）现象。结果显示模型间的一致性显著高于人类读者间的一致性，但这一效应随模型规模呈梯度分布，小模型与人类水平相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于语言模型同质化的研究依赖于为研究专门收集的人类判断作为基准，这使人类侧数据成为研究设计的人为产物——众包工人在给定模型指令的情况下实际上是在"运行模型的提示"。本文旨在用独立于研究设计的人类参考数据来客观测量模型同质化。

Method: 使用2523个读者在120个网页文档上的自主标记集（平台默认关闭他人标记叠加）作为人类基准。一致性定义为两个大小匹配的句子集的重叠减去各自在深度和长度带内重采样的预期重叠。通过18个模型组别（涵盖11个供应商、3个国家、两种权重制度）的153对模型配对进行测量，并验证空模型的校准性（随机基线落在零附近0.006以内）。

Result: 在中等文档中，每位读者标注14个句子（共70句），两名读者共享4.1个句子，而两个模型共享8.7个句子。153对模型配对的中位数为+0.093，远超人类基准+0.040，其中99对完全高于人类区间。两个领先实验室的前沿模型达到+0.203，是GPT-4o自身两次调用一致性（约0.10）的两倍。该效应呈梯度分布：最小模型与人类水平一致。但样本外测试显示，四个在分析后发布的模型均未超过人类区间。

Conclusion: 模型同质化效应真实存在且呈梯度分布，但不是由于确定性、提示措辞、程序、供应商或路由所致。然而该效应具有程序依赖性：多重数值取决于测量程序，但排序稳定。更关键的是，通过模拟多个模型生成的"人群"并不等于多个人群——模型的多样性是表面的而非真实的。

Abstract: Claims that language models homogenise are usually measured against human judgements collected for the study, which makes the human side an artifact of the design: a crowdworker given the model's instruction is running the model's prompt. We measure convergence against a human reference nobody built for the purpose -- 2,523 reader mark sets across 120 web documents, produced by people highlighting for their own reasons on a platform where the overlay of others' marks is off by default.
  Agreement is the overlap between two size-matched sentence sets minus the overlap expected when each is resampled within its own depth-and-length bands. The null's calibration is demonstrated, not asserted: every pair involving a random baseline lands within 0.006 of zero. On the median document each party names 14 sentences of 70; two readers share 4.1 and two models 8.7.
  Across 18 model arms spanning 11 vendors, 3 countries and both weight regimes, the median of 153 model pairs is +0.093 against a human yardstick of +0.040, and 99 sit entirely above the human interval. Two frontier models from rival labs reach +0.203, twice what GPT-4o agrees with itself on a second call. The effect is not determinism, prompt wording, procedure, vendor or routing, and it is graded: the smallest models agree at the human level. No model agrees with readers detectably more than a reader does, and at equal depth and length no surface feature separates their choices.
  The multiples are procedure-dependent and the ordering is not: models are cut to their sharpest set while a reader's is a random draw from what they marked, and blunting the models alike halves the gap without closing it. Tested out of sample on four models released after this analysis, against predictions fixed beforehand, none clears the human interval. A population simulated from several models is not several populations.

</details>


### [164] [Bridging the Question-Answer Gap in Retrieval-Augmented Generation: Hypothetical Prompt Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29402)
*Domen Vake,Jernej Vičič,Aleksandar Tošić*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HyPE框架将假设性内容的生成从查询阶段转移到索引阶段，通过为每个数据块预计算多个假设提示并嵌入块本身，将检索转化为问题-问题匹配任务，在无额外延迟的情况下显著提升检索精度和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中用户查询与文档文本之间的风格差异仍然是一个持续挑战。现有的运行时解决方案（如HyDE）虽然试图改善对齐，但在查询时引入了额外的计算开销，影响系统效率。

Method: 提出Hypothetical Prompt Embeddings (HyPE)框架，核心创新是将假设内容的生成从查询时间转移到索引阶段。具体做法是为每个数据块预计算多个假设提示，用块本身替代提示进行嵌入，使检索任务从查询-文档匹配转变为问题-问题匹配，从而完全绕开运行时的合成答案生成需求。

Result: 在六个常见数据集上的实验表明，相比标准方法，HyPE能将检索上下文精度提升最多42个百分点，声明召回率提升最多45个百分点，且与重排序、多向量检索、查询分解等RAG进阶技术兼容。

Conclusion: HyPE通过索引阶段的假设提示预生成，在避免引入查询延迟的同时增强了查询与相关上下文之间的对齐，为RAG系统的效率和效果提供了双赢的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems synergize retrieval mechanisms with generative language models to enhance the accuracy and relevance of responses. However, bridging the style gap between user queries and relevant information in document text remains a persistent challenge in retrieval-augmented systems, often addressed by runtime solutions (e.g., Hypothetical Document Embeddings (HyDE)) that attempt to improve alignment but introduce extra computational overhead at query time. To address these challenges, we propose Hypothetical Prompt Embeddings (HyPE), a framework that shifts the generation of hypothetical content from query time to the indexing phase. By precomputing multiple hypothetical prompts for each data chunk and embedding the chunk in place of the prompt, HyPE transforms retrieval into a question-question matching task, bypassing the need for runtime synthetic answer generation. This approach does not introduce latency but also strengthens the alignment between queries and relevant context. Our experimental results on six common datasets show that HyPE can improve retrieval context precision by up to 42 percentage points and claim recall by up to 45 percentage points, compared to standard approaches, while remaining compatible with re-ranking, multi-vector retrieval, query decomposition, and other RAG advancements

</details>


### [165] [QASP: Query-Adaptive Robust Vector Search Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29606)
*Hakan Ferhatosmanoglu,Kushal Kumar,Tal Wagner,Andy Warfield*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: QASP是一种查询自适应的向量搜索策略方法，通过单次回归预测每条查询的完整召回进度曲线，从而为任意召回目标推导搜索策略，避免搜索过程中的迭代模型调用。该方法在保持高召回率的同时显著降低计算成本，实验表明能达到99%召回率且减少80%的数据访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量搜索面临的核心挑战是：固定搜索参数在不同查询间存在显著的性能差异，而传统的平均召回率评估方式掩盖了这种逐查询的不均衡性。需要一种能够自适应不同查询特征的搜索策略，以在保持高召回率的同时最小化计算开销。

Method: QASP通过以下方式实现：1）使用单次前置监督回归预测每条查询的完整召回进度曲线，而非在搜索过程中迭代调用模型；2）通过预测归一化召回值并使用尺度不变特征，使其能泛化到不同召回目标、索引配置和数据集；3）利用细粒度进度预测实现轻量级反应式补充机制，根据预测与观测偏差调整搜索深度，无需额外推理。

Result: 理论证明方面，QASP需要有限的训练样本（与数据集大小和维度无关），其损失与任何固定策略的不可约下界之间的差距可忽略不计，且相比固定探测的数据访问节省随内在维度指数增长。实验方面，QASP显著降低了召回方差和目标偏差，提高了查询满意度，可扩展到大数据和层次索引而无需重新训练，实现了99%召回率的同时减少80%的数据访问。

Conclusion: QASP通过查询自适应的搜索策略生成方式，有效解决了传统固定参数向量搜索的性能不均衡问题，实现了高召回率与低计算成本的兼顾，且具有良好的可扩展性和泛化能力。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge of vector search is achieving consistently high recall while minimizing computational costs. Fixed search parameters cause significant performance variance across queries, and conventional evaluation on average recall masks these per-query disparities. We introduce QASP (Query-Adaptive robust vector Search Policy), which predicts the complete recall progression curve per query via a single upfront supervised regression, from which a search policy is derived for any recall target; this avoids iterative model invocations during search or separate predictors per target. By predicting normalized recall values with scale-invariant features and pre-search inference, QASP generalizes across recall targets, index configurations, and datasets. Its fine-grained progress predictions further enable a lightweight reactive complement that adjusts search depth based on predicted-versus-observed deviations without additional inference. We prove that QASP requires a finite training sample independent of dataset size and dimensionality, that its loss exceeds the irreducible lower bound of any fixed policy by a vanishing margin, and that its data access savings over fixed probing grow exponentially in intrinsic dimensionality. Experimentally, QASP achieves significantly lower recall variance and deviation from target, higher query satisfaction rate, and scales to large data and hierarchical indices without retraining, achieving 99% recall with 80% less data access.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [NeuroSynth: A Biologically Inspired Continual Reinforcement Learning Architecture for Mitigating Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28663)
*Yash Kini*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroSynth，一种受大脑双路径记忆机制启发的持续强化学习架构，通过"计划"和"习惯"两条通路结合回放与知识蒸馏来缓解灾难性遗忘。在三个连续导航任务上与PPO和EWC对比，NeuroSynth显著保留了更多早期任务知识，且最终任务表现优于EWC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在连续学习条件下会出现灾难性遗忘——学习新任务会覆盖之前获得的知识。生物学习系统通过海马体的快速编码和大脑皮层的缓慢巩固这一互补记忆过程来减少这种干扰，作者希望借鉴这一生物机制来解决持续强化学习中的遗忘问题。

Method: NeuroSynth采用双路径巩固机制：将快速任务获取（"计划"路径）与长期保留（"习惯"路径）分离，并结合回放（replay）和知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）技术。在非重复访问的持续学习环境中，通过三个连续导航任务（目标位置不断改变）进行评估，与PPO和EWC基线进行比较。

Result: 在六个独立随机种子下，NeuroSynth显著优于PPO：任务A成功率达18.00%（PPO仅0.33%，p=0.014929，Cohen's d=1.49），任务B成功率达35.33%（PPO为0.00%，p=0.002376，Cohen's d=2.31）。最终任务C表现优于EWC（9.00% vs 2.00%，Cohen's d=0.56），但差异未达到统计显著性（p=0.226643）。

Conclusion: 生物启发的巩固机制可能改善持续强化学习系统的稳定性-可塑性平衡。NeuroSynth证明了双路径记忆整合结合回放与知识蒸馏能够有效缓解灾难性遗忘，尽管在最终任务上的优势尚需更大规模实验来确认其统计显著性。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems often perform well on isolated tasks but struggle under continual learning conditions, where training on new tasks can overwrite previously acquired knowledge, a failure mode known as catastrophic forgetting. Biological learning systems reduce this interference through complementary memory processes involving rapid hippocampal encoding and slower cortical consolidation. This study introduces NeuroSynth, a brain-inspired continual reinforcement learning architecture designed to mitigate catastrophic forgetting through a dual-pathway consolidation mechanism. NeuroSynth separates rapid task acquisition from long-term retention using distinct "plan" and "habit" pathways combined with replay and knowledge distillation. NeuroSynth was evaluated against Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) across three sequential navigation tasks with changing goal locations in a non-revisitation continual learning setting. Across six independent seeds, NeuroSynth preserved substantially more early-task knowledge than PPO after sequential training, achieving 18.00% Task A success rate compared to 0.33% for PPO (p = 0.014929, Cohen's d = 1.49) and 35.33% Task B success rate compared to 0.00% for PPO (p = 0.002376, Cohen's d = 2.31). NeuroSynth also demonstrated higher final Task C performance than EWC, achieving 9.00% compared to 2.00% (p = 0.226643, Cohen's d = 0.56), indicating a moderate but not statistically significant advantage. These findings suggest that biologically inspired consolidation mechanisms may improve the stability-plasticity balance in continual reinforcement learning systems.

</details>


### [167] [Analysis of Memory-Runtime Trade-offs in Caching Strategies for Genetic Programming Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29116)
*Jiaming Shi,Kei Sen Fong,Mehul Motani*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文对遗传编程符号回归（GPSR）中的缓存机制进行了全面的比较分析，在合成与真实数据集上评估了不同缓存策略的性能，并基于无限缓存下的键值使用频率研究，为缓存大小配置和策略选择提供了实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPSR在进化过程中需要反复评估整个表达式或其子表达式，导致计算开销巨大。虽然已有缓存机制被用来减少冗余计算，但以往研究通常只采用单一缓存策略，缺乏对不同策略的对比性能以及内存与运行时间之间权衡的深入理解。因此，需要一个系统性的分析来指导缓存策略的选择与配置。

Method: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上，对多种缓存机制进行全面的实证比较分析；同时，在无限缓存假设下研究键值的实际使用频率，据此分析最优缓存大小的设定；最后根据计算和内存约束，提出可操作的缓存策略配置指南。

Result: 研究发现复杂的缓存机制需要达到一定的缓存大小才能实现计算时间的缩减；而轻量级缓存策略（如LRU和FIFO）能够显著减少适应度评估的计算时间，而适应度评估是整体运行时间的重要组成部分。

Conclusion: 对于计算资源受限或内存受限的场景，轻量级缓存策略（尤其是FIFO）是高效且实用的选择；复杂缓存机制需要足够大的缓存空间才能发挥优势。本文为GPSR缓存配置提供了依据数据驱动的最佳实践指导。

Abstract: Genetic Programming Symbolic Regression (GPSR) generates mathematical expressions to model input-output relationships using an evolutionary process. A significant challenge in GPSR lies in the repeated evaluation of entire expressions or their sub-expression, which inflates computational runtime. To address this inefficiency, caching mechanisms have been employed to reduce redundant computations. However, prior studies predominantly employ a single caching strategy, offering limited insights into their comparative performance or memory-runtime trade-offs. In this paper, we present a comprehensive analysis of caching mechanisms for GPSR on synthetic and real-world datasets. We also include an empirical study of key-value usage frequencies under an infinitely large cache, offering insights into optimal cache sizing. Furthermore, we provide actionable guidelines for configuring caching strategies based on computational and memory constraints. Our findings indicate that complex caching mechanisms necessitate a minimum cache size to achieve computational time reductions. Conversely, lightweight caching strategies, such as Least Recently Used (LRU) and, notably, First-In-First-Out (FIFO), can significantly decrease computation time for fitness evaluations, which are a substantial component of the overall runtime.

</details>


### [168] [Linear Proposal Operators and Stochastic Search Geometry in SOMA and Differential Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29228)
*Vojtěch Novák,Ivan Zelinka*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种算子-选择分解方法，将群体优化算法中的目标无关变异与边界修复和适应度依赖选择分离，统一分析SOMA和DE的候选生成几何结构，并基于此设计改进的SOMA变体，在BBOB基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体与进化算法通常被视为完整的程序化系统进行分析，其中非线性的选择、替换和适应机制掩盖了候选生成内部的更简单结构。作者希望揭示候选生成操作符的底层几何结构，以便更好地解释和设计基于群体的优化器。

Method: 提出算子-选择分解框架，将变异（目标无关）与边界修复和选择（适应度依赖）分离。在领导者相对坐标下分析SOMA和DE的算子几何：推导SOMA候选生成的仿射/线性性质及均值、协方差、期望步长、活动维度等闭式表达式；对DE/rand/1/bin推导有限种群差分变异矩及强制坐标二项交叉的协方差效应。基于分析设计几何控制和旋转感知的SOMA变体及自适应种群缩减iSOMA。

Result: 精确枚举和蒙特卡洛实验验证了分析恒等式；在完整无噪声BBOB基准上，算子引导的SOMA变体显著优于标准SOMA，并在多个维度-预算区间与成熟DE方法竞争。

Conclusion: 提案级算子分析能够同时支持基于群体的优化器的解释与设计，为算法改进提供了新的理论指导路径。

Abstract: Swarm and evolutionary algorithms are usually analyzed as complete procedural systems in which nonlinear selection, replacement, and adaptation obscure simpler structure within candidate generation. This paper introduces an operator--selection factorization that separates objective-independent variation from boundary repair and fitness-dependent selection, and uses it to study the proposal geometry of the Self-Organizing Migrating Algorithm (SOMA) and Differential Evolution (DE). The canonical SOMA proposal is shown to be affine in the search space and exactly linear in an augmented migrant--leader state. In leader-relative coordinates, the resulting operator provides a direct interpretation of interpolation, projection, overshooting, and coordinate masking. Under Bernoulli perturbation masks, we derive closed-form expressions for the proposal mean, covariance, expected squared step length, expected squared distance from the leader, active dimensionality, and coordinate coverage. For canonical DE/rand/1/bin, we derive the finite-population moments of differential mutation and characterize the additional covariance and coordinate dependence induced by forced-coordinate binomial crossover. Exact enumeration and Monte Carlo experiments verify the analytical identities and quantify the effects of mask conditioning, boundary repair, and fitness-based selection. The analysis further motivates geometry-controlled and rotation-aware SOMA variants, together with an adaptive population-reducing extension of iSOMA. Experiments on the complete noiseless BBOB benchmark show that these operator-guided variants substantially improve upon canonical SOMA and are competitive with established DE methods in several dimension--budget regimes. The results demonstrate how proposal-level operator analysis can support both the interpretation and design of population-based optimizers.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Topology-Aware Data Movement for Disaggregated GPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28633)
*Sanjeev Rao Ganjihal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM推理中KV缓存在prefill和decode GPU池之间传输的网络瓶颈问题，提出了一种拓扑感知的传输编排器，利用互连层级感知、流水线传输、NVLink域感知放置和CXL 3.0内存扩展器三种机制，实现3-18倍传输延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式LLM推理系统（如DistServe、Splitwise、Mooncake）在分离prefill和decode时，均使用统一的RDMA传输，忽略了GPU之间的物理拓扑差异（NVLink 900GB/s、InfiniBand 50GB/s、TCP 12.5GB/s，差异达72倍），导致KV缓存传输无法充分利用高带宽路径，造成严重的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出拓扑感知的传输编排器，包含三个核心机制：(1) 流水线化的逐层传输，将KV缓存传输与进行中的prefill计算重叠，隐藏60-85%的延迟；(2) NVLink域感知的placement策略，针对Mixture-of-Experts模型联合优化expert调度与KV缓存局部性；(3) 利用CXL 3.0内存扩展器作为共享溢出层，提供6倍容量且延迟比NVMe低86倍。

Result: 由于缺乏多节点异构互连集群和CXL 3.0硬件（学术资源不可及且GPU云尚未提供），论文采用分析带宽模型、组件实现和跨三种架构的投影分析，结果显示相比统一RDMA可降低3-18倍传输延迟。

Conclusion: 现有LLM推理系统忽视了数据中心互连拓扑的异构性，本文提出的拓扑感知方法能够显著提升KV缓存传输效率，但完整评估受限于硬件条件，需要未来在实际CXL 3.0和多级互连环境中验证。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM inference creates a datacenter networking problem that no existing system solves correctly. When prefill and decode run on separate GPU pools, the KV cache must be transferred between them. For a 70B model this is 2.6 GB per request, exceeding 100 GB/s aggregate at production scale. Yet DistServe, Splitwise, and Mooncake all use uniform RDMA, ignoring that bandwidth between two GPUs varies by 72x depending on their physical relationship: 900 GB/s via NVLink within a domain, 50 GB/s via InfiniBand across nodes, 12.5 GB/s via TCP across data centers. We design a topology-aware transfer orchestrator that discovers interconnect hierarchy at startup and selects optimal transport per transfer. Three mechanisms work together: (1) pipelined layer-by-layer transfer that overlaps transmission with ongoing prefill, hiding 60 to 85 percent of latency behind computation; (2) NVLink domain-aware placement for Mixture-of-Experts models that co-optimizes expert dispatch with KV cache locality; and (3) CXL 3.0 memory expanders as a shared overflow tier providing 6x capacity at 86x lower latency than NVMe. Full evaluation requires multi-node clusters with heterogeneous interconnects and CXL 3.0 hardware that is beyond academic resources and not yet available in GPU clouds. We present analytical bandwidth models, component implementations, and projected analysis across three architectures showing 3 to 18x transfer latency reduction over uniform RDMA.

</details>


### [170] [Learning Optimal Dynamic Matching via Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28925)
*Genta Okada,Shunya Noda,Junpei Komiyama,Akira Matsushita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的价值框架，用于在有限演化加权图上的动态匹配问题，通过事件时间归约和图神经网络值近似，学习状态依赖的动态匹配策略，在基准实验中优于即时和阈值贪心规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态匹配市场需要在"现在匹配获得价值"与"保留参与者等待未来更好机会"之间做权衡，而现有方法难以有效处理这种时序决策问题。

Method: 1) 建立无限时域连续时间模型，包含随机到达、节点类型转移、边实现和外生退出；2) 证明事件时间归约（只需在每次外生事件后决策）；3) 证明最优边级Q函数可由单个延续价值函数刻画，将学习对象从状态-动作价值降为图价值；4) 用图神经网络近似价值，用时序差分学习训练，并用于前向贪心匹配启发式。

Result: 在二元类型基准中，学习到的策略显著优于即时和阈值贪心规则；在肾配对捐赠基准中，在退出不可预测时与即时贪心表现相当，在预警可靠时恢复患者匹配逻辑，并在中间预警概率下优于两种贪心策略。

Conclusion: 残差图价值学习能够生成适应实际连通性和退出信息的动态匹配策略，为动态匹配市场提供了有效的决策框架。

Abstract: Dynamic matching markets require decisions about whom to match and when: matching now yields value but removes participants who may create better future opportunities. We develop a value-based reinforcement-learning framework for this problem on finite, evolving weighted graphs. We study an infinite-horizon continuous-time model with stochastic arrivals, node-type transitions, edge realizations, and exogenous exits. We prove an event-time reduction: without loss of optimality, the planner acts immediately after each exogenous event and then waits for the next one. We further show that the optimal edge-wise $Q$-function is characterized by a single continuation-value function on post-decision residual graphs, reducing the learned object from state-action values to graph values. Exact action selection still requires combinatorial matching optimization; we approximate the value with a graph neural network, train it by temporal-difference learning, and use it in a forward-greedy matching heuristic. In a binary-type benchmark, the learned policy substantially outperforms immediate and threshold-greedy rules by preserving common nodes for rare arrivals of valuable matches while forming lower-value matches only in thick pools. In a kidney paired donation benchmark, it performs similarly to immediate greedy when exits are unpredictable, recovers the logic of patient matching when warnings are reliable, and outperforms the better of Immediate Greedy and Patient Greedy across intermediate warning probabilities. These results show that residual-graph value learning yields state-dependent dynamic matching policies that adapt to realized connectivity and exit information.

</details>


### [171] [Sensitivity Analysis of GRU, LSTM and Transformer Encoder in Classification of Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28665)
*Bidhya Shrestha,Christos Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种序列分类模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer）基于车辆遥测数据识别Level 2自动驾驶系统（Comma Openpilot、Tesla Autopilot、Cadillac Super Cruise）的效果，并提出了一个模块化鲁棒性评估框架来模拟真实的遥测数据退化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶系统日益普及，软件定义车辆可能同时运行多个原生和后装自动驾驶系统。独立验证哪个自动驾驶系统正在运行对于安全监控、法规合规、保险评估和异常检测至关重要。

Method: 1）使用三种序列分类模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer encoder）基于车辆遥测数据识别自动驾驶系统，并采用威胁匹配训练策略。2）提出模块化鲁棒性评估框架，包括五种损坏类型（加性高斯白噪声+累积漂移、相关跨通道噪声、时间抖动）和五个严重等级（L1-L5），同时模拟二进制事件信号的突发丢失、延迟转换、虚假切换和跨特征不一致。

Result: 三种模型在干净数据上均表现良好（macro F1为0.90-0.93），威胁匹配训练达到0.904-0.916的macro F1且仅带来较小的干净数据性能损失。事件级别损坏影响较小（L5时macro F1仍≥0.87），但时间抖动导致macro F1骤降至0.44-0.50，三种模型均严重退化。

Conclusion: 事件级损坏对模型影响有限，但时间抖动是主要脆弱点，对所有三种模型都造成灾难性性能下降，说明时间相关特征的鲁棒性是未来需要重点解决的方向。

Abstract: Automated driving systems (ADSs) are becoming ubiquitous. Future Software Defined Vehicles (SDVs) may be able to run multiple ADSs, both native and aftermarket such as Comma.ai's Openpilot. Monitoring systems to independently verify which automated driving system is active are important for safety monitoring, regulatory compliance, insurance assessment, and anomaly detection. In this paper, we first evaluate the effectiveness of three sequence-based classification models: Gated Recurrent Units (GRU), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and a Transformer encoder model for identifying Level 2 automated driving systems using vehicle telematics data alone: Comma Openpilot, Tesla Autopilot, and Cadillac Super Cruise, along with manual driving. All three models achieve strong clean-data performance with macro F1-scores of 0.92 (GRU), 0.90 (LSTM), and 0.93 (Transformer encoder model) when trained on clean data; threat-matched training yields 0.904-0.916 macro F1 with only a modest clean-data penalty. Second, we introduce a modular robustness evaluation framework that simulates realistic telematics degradation through five corruption families at five severity levels (L1-L5). Continuous channels are perturbed using additive white Gaussian noise with cumulative drift, correlated cross-channel noise, and temporal jitter. Binary event signals are subjected to burst loss, delayed transitions, spurious toggles and cross-feature inconsistencies inspired by communication errors. Robustness is measured using macro-F1, which gives equal weight to each class and is suitable for imbalanced multiclass evaluation. Our evaluation reveals a sharp failure-mode split: event-level corruptions reduce macro-F1 only slightly (greater than equal to 0.87 at L5), while temporal jitter collapses macro-F1 to 0.44-0.50 across GRU, LSTM, and Transformer encoder model.

</details>


### [172] [Guarantees on Dynamical System Distinguishability for LLM Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28667)
*Mohamed Akrout,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析了将LLM响应分类建模为黑盒动力系统（DS）的方法，通过将分类问题形式化为两个随机线性DS之间的二元假设检验，证明了仅基于静态分布的分类存在准确率下限、基于DS的分类错误率随序列长度指数衰减，并给出了跨嵌入泛化的条件与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于动力系统的LLM响应分类方法在经验上取得了成功，但缺乏对其为什么有效、性能如何随序列长度扩展、以及何时能跨嵌入模型迁移的理论理解。

Method: 将分类任务形式化为两个随机线性DS之间的二元假设检验；分析两个DS平稳边际分布之间的全变差距离；推导基于DS分类的误分类概率随序列长度L的指数衰减率（由动力判别量δ²控制）；引入两个嵌入模型之间的近似交织条件，并建立可迁移判别性关于交织映射最小奇异值的下界。

Result: 1) 两个DS在平稳边际分布上的全变差距离可以任意小，即使其动力学差异显著，这为忽略token动力学的分类器提供了准确率下限；2) 基于DS的分类误分类概率随序列长度L指数衰减，衰减率由δ²（两个DS的谱距离）决定；3) 跨嵌入泛化可通过近似交织条件刻画，可迁移判别性受交织映射最小奇异值的下界约束。

Conclusion: 这些理论结果解释了基于DS分类的经验性能，并激励利用DS理论分析AI系统的进一步研究，这与用AI建模动力系统的常见方法形成对比。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that classifying large language models (LLMs)' responses can be distinguished by modeling token embeddings as trajectories of a black-box dynamical system (DS) and comparing prediction residuals of two DSs. Despite the empirical success of this dynamical approach, a theoretical understanding of why it works, how well it scales as a function of the token sequence, and when it transfers across embedding models remains lacking. We address these questions by formalizing the classification task as a binary hypothesis test between two stochastic linear DSs. We show that the total variation distance between the stationary marginal distributions of the two DSs can be arbitrarily small even when the dynamics differ substantially, which provides a fundamental accuracy floor for any classifier that ignores token dynamics. We then show that the misclassification probability of DS-based classification decays exponentially in the sequence length $L$, with the decay governed by a dynamical discriminability quantity $δ^2$ that captures the spectral distance between the two DSs. We also characterize cross-embedding generalization by introducing an approximate intertwining condition between embedding models and establishing a lower bound on the transferable discriminability in terms of the intertwining map's smallest singular value. Together, these results explain the empirical performance of DS-based classification and motivate further investigation into using DS theory to analyze AI systems, in contrast to the more common approach of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [173] [SILVA Networks as Structured Implicit Layers and Vector Attractors via Dynamic Interaction Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28989)
*Jose Luis Lima de Jesus Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SILVA网络，一种结构化的隐式神经层架构，将输入刺激、局部交互、全局交互、阻尼和读出分离为固定点架构中的独立组件，可用于图像、分子、引文网络和长程图基准任务。实验显示各组件在不同任务中有不同的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式神经层将所有影响因素（直接输入、局部结构、全局上下文）吸收到单一固定点更新中，难以区分各自贡献，也难以诊断内部的交互动态。

Method: 提出SILVA Networks（结构化隐式层与向量吸引子），在单一固定点架构中显式分离刺激、局部交互、全局交互、阻尼和读出五个组件，并通过领域特定定义节点、邻域和全局摘要来适配不同数据模态。

Result: 实验表明：局部交互在图任务中起主要作用；MNIST在测试容量下从循环中获益有限；长程节点分类基准中全局交互的收益最明显。

Conclusion: SILVA提供了可训练、可消融、可可视化和可诊断的隐式表示，其内部交互动态可被明确分析和理解。

Abstract: Many learning problems require representations that reconcile direct input, nearby structure, and broader context. In implicit neural layers, these influences are usually absorbed into a single fixed-point update, making it hard to identify what enters from the stimulus, what propagates locally, what comes from global context, and what is produced by solver dynamics. Here we introduce SILVA Networks, Structured Implicit Layers and Vector Attractors via Dynamic Interaction Fields. SILVA separates stimulus, local interaction, global interaction, damping, and readout inside one fixed-point architecture. The same template is instantiated for images, molecules, citation networks, and long-range graph benchmarks through domain-specific definitions of nodes, neighborhoods, and global summaries. Experiments and ablations show task-dependent roles for these terms: local interactions are load-bearing in the graph tasks, MNIST gains little from recurrence at the tested capacity, and the clearest global benefit appears in a long-range node-classification benchmark.
